# 🛡️ Daily Audit Report: 2026-09-07

**Product HEAD audited:** `3d92f9d9cfbf7fa4e02b763eeb62a131d494f040` (merge #101 — identical to `origin/dev` and to the 2026-08-20 through 2026-09-06 audits)
**Audit branch:** `cursor/daily-repository-audit-bdfd`
**Scope:** Holistic security / bug / architecture / debt re-verification; secret, DDL, auth-guard, XSS/SQLi, npm, unguarded-controller, login-throttle, login-password-skip (including falsy empty-string), register identity-binding, root-admin bootstrap (including identity-source split), stats-surface (including stats/details PII dump), JWT-refresh-role, chat write-path, Firebase JWKS audience, JwtAuthGuard rate-limit identifier, Google OAuth access-token fallback, profile ownership/password IDOR (including root-admin carve-out), committed credential fingerprints, admin-files ACL, collections static-route shadowing, rate-limit custom-rule / stress-test, donation actor-binding / donation IDOR / donation-detail PII / donation-by-user dump / donation list `d.*` dump, task log-hours spoof, task hours-report IDOR, task-by-id / tree email dump, items-delivery + dedicated-items static-route shadowing, community-challenges daily-tracker IDOR, private-list leak via `creator_id`, comment update-delete actor spoof, posts friends-feed viewer impersonation, rides public driver PII, community-members `contact_info` dump, collections `userId=all` JSONB dump, CRM `SELECT *` contact dump, unauthenticated ride POST/PUT/DELETE including auto-provision of `user_profiles`, hidden-post bypass on user-posts / by-id / comments, and **hidden-post liker roster on GET :postId/likes**
**SRS mapping:** NFR §3.1–3.2 (`docs/SSOT/SRS/03-non-functional-requirements.md`); auth §2.1, users §2.2, donations §2.3, rides §2.4, posts §2.5, challenges §2.8, chat §2.7, stats §2.10 (`docs/SSOT/SRS/functional/*`); backend §5; gaps §10
**Mode:** Report-only (no product code fixes in this pass — blast radius of confirmed issues is multi-module; prior daily-audit PRs #105–#153 remain open drafts and unmerged to `dev`)
**Delta vs 2026-09-06:** No product commits. All previously confirmed critical items re-verified at the same line numbers. New first-class finding: unauthenticated `GET /api/posts/:postId/likes` (1463–1520) lists liker `id` / `name` / `avatar_url` with no join on `posts.status`. After an owner hide (`POST /api/posts/:postId/hide` sets `status='hidden'`), the public feed omits the post but the liker roster remains enumerable. Complements the 2026-09-06 hidden-post bypass on user-posts / by-id / comments (the 09-06 remediation sentence already named likes; this pass confirms the roster endpoint as a concrete social-graph leak). npm: root `npm audit --json` was non-deterministic **56 → 100**; `--workspaces` succeeded at **99**; critical still **4** (`handlebars`, `shell-quote`, `tar`, `websocket-driver`). Files >200 LOC: **197**. Request-path XSS/SQLi patterns remain absent (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` none; no runtime `eval(` in apps); rides/items-delivery `ORDER BY` columns are allowlisted.

---

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. If Admin SDK is initialized and no local row matches, `tryAutoCreateUserFromFirebase` calls `admin.auth().getUser(firebase_uid)` **without an ID token** and provisions a KC user + tokens from that UID. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body.

 * **Primary mobile login accepts email-only (password verification skipped, including empty string).** `POST /api/users/login` verifies argon2 **only when both** `loginData.password` and `user.password_hash` are truthy (223–231). Omitting `password`, sending `password: ""`, or targeting a null hash falls through to `success: true` and returns email, phone, roles, and `parent_manager_id`. `/auth/login` correctly rejects missing hash and invalid passwords. Combined with resolve-id, the documented mobile sequence is an email-only takeover. Contrasts SRS §2.1.2. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (116–118); `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (190–263, especially 223–231) -> Action: Always require a non-empty password; fail closed if hash is missing; never return `success: true` without `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated root-admin bootstrap via committed fallback email (identity-source split).** `KC_ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL_FALLBACK` is committed in `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/register` with that email sets `initialRoles = ["user", "admin"]` (101–102). Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/login` with the same email runs `array_append(roles, 'admin')` **before** the optional password check (212–220), then succeeds without a password. `getRootAdminEmail()` reads only `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` (empty if unset) and is used solely to skip `roles` writes. The PUT role/hierarchy **block** (281–297) compares a **hardcoded Gmail literal**, not the env var and not the shared constant. Setting `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` to a different mailbox does not stop bootstrap of the fallback address. - File: `apps/api/src/config/kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`; `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (101–102, 212–231); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (52–56, 281–297, 349–361) -> Action: Single source of truth for root-admin identity; require verified secret/env match plus password; never mutate roles before `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated register binds attacker-chosen Firebase UID.** `POST /api/users/register` has no guard. INSERT writes `userData.firebase_uid || userData.id || null` into `user_profiles.firebase_uid` (139). Password is optional (97–99). The victim’s later Firebase ID-token path attaches the attacker’s KC row. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (97–99, 139) -> Action: Ignore client `firebase_uid`/`id` on public register; require a verified Firebase ID token before binding.

 * **JWT-gated profile mass-assignment + password IDOR (`PUT /api/users/:id`).** `UpdateUserBody` accepts `password`, `roles`, and `firebase_uid`. Controller uses `JwtAuthGuard` only — **no ownership check and no `req.user` binding**. Lookup matches UUID **or** email / `firebase_uid` / `google_id` (267). Root-admin block covers roles/hierarchy only; `password` and `firebase_uid` are still written (303–347). Any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) can reset any other user’s password or steal their Firebase identity. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 267, 281–361) -> Action: Bind `:id` to `req.user.userId` unless admin; strip `password`/`roles`/`firebase_uid` from the public DTO; extend the root-admin carve-out to password and identity fields.

 * **Unauthenticated user directory and profile PII.** `GET /api/users`, `GET /api/users/:id`, `GET /api/users/search`, `GET /api/users/stats/summary`, `GET /api/users/:id/activities`, and `GET /api/users/:id/stats` have no guards. `getUserById` / `searchUsers` return email, phone, and roles. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 101–123, 135–163) -> Action: Require auth (and minimize fields) on list/search/profile reads.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** JWT-gated promote/demote/set-manager/manage-hierarchy trust body `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. `setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` **skips the admin check** when the id is omitted or not found. `manageHierarchy` applies add/remove with no JWT binding. Unauthenticated `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, and `GET :id/hierarchy` leak the org tree. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` -> Action: Bind actor to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard`; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **JWT refresh copies stale roles (SRS §2.1.4 violation).** `refreshAccessToken` copies `payload.roles` from the refresh token and never reloads `user_profiles.roles`. Demoted admins keep elevated roles until the 30-day refresh token expires or is revoked. Redis existence **is** checked in `verifyToken`. HMAC compare uses `!==` not `timingSafeEqual` (308). Missing `exp` is treated as not-expired (`payload.exp < now` is false when `exp` is undefined). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/jwt.service.ts` (146–177, 308) -> Action: Reload roles from DB on refresh; reject tokens without `exp`; use `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **Three additional unauthenticated login paths besides resolve-id.** (1) Firebase JWKS fallback accepts any Google-signed Firebase ID token unless `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID` is set (`firebase-admin.service.ts` 11–14, 177–181); fabricated `exp=now+3600` if claim missing (216–219). (2) `POST /auth/google` falls back to `GET oauth2/v3/userinfo` with a raw Bearer access token, hardcodes `emailVerified: true`, and skips `aud` binding (613–698). (3) JwtAuthGuard Firebase fallback (117–134) then looks up `user_profiles` by `firebase_uid`. Combined with register/profile `firebase_uid` mass-assignment this is account takeover. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/firebase-admin.service.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/auth.controller.ts` (613–698); `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` -> Action: Require `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID`; reject access-token fallback; never bind Firebase UID from an unauthenticated body.

 * **JwtAuthGuard shared HS256-header rate-limit bucket + unauthenticated plant-block.** `checkRateLimit(token.substring(0, 16), "api_access")` — issued header is always `{alg:HS256,typ:JWT}` so first 16 chars are always the same prefix. 100 req/60s + 5-min block is global for all JWT users. Thrown as `UnauthorizedException` (401); mobile interceptor clears session on 401. Unauthenticated `POST /rate-limit/custom` plants `blocked:{customKey}:{identifier}` with attacker `blockDurationMs`. Unauthenticated `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` deletes window and block keys. `POST /rate-limit/stress-test` honors unbounded `delayMs` (HTTP timeout 30s). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (96–114); `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (61–97, 233–264) -> Action: Rate-limit by `userId`/session, not JWT header bytes; require admin on `/rate-limit/*`; cap `delayMs`.

 * **ThrottlerModule is configured but not globally enforced.** `ThrottlerModule.forRoot` is present (`app.module.ts` 72–77) with **zero** `APP_GUARD` matches under `apps/api/src`. Only Auth/Notifications opt in to `ThrottlerGuard`. Primary mobile `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` are unthrottled. Contrasts SRS §3.1 (global 60 req/60s). - File: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118) -> Action: Register `ThrottlerGuard` as `APP_GUARD`; apply stricter limits on login/register.

 * **Unauthenticated chat guest path + sender impersonation.** Class-level `OptionalAuthGuard` always returns `true` (`jwt-auth.guard.ts` 338). Unauthenticated `GET` conversations/messages by user/conversation id; unauthenticated `POST /api/chat/messages` uses body `sender_id`. Authenticated mismatch is rejected (450–455). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 267–268, 378–387, 441–465) -> Action: Require JWT on all chat reads/writes; bind `sender_id` to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Follow spoof.** JWT-gated `POST`/`DELETE /api/users/:id/follow` writes `followData.follower_id` from the body, not `req.user`. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts` (19–34); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (165–180) -> Action: Bind follower to `req.user.userId`.

 * **JWT-gated but unscoped tasks list + by-id email dump.** `GET /api/tasks` is JWT-only and unscoped. `GET :id` / `:id/subtasks` / `:id/tree` (191–249) call `getTaskById` which embeds `creator_details` and `assignees_details` emails (`tasks-read.service.ts` 48–59) with no owner/assignee/admin check. `GET /api/tasks/hours-report/:managerId` (568–587) returns org-tree names/hours/task titles; catch returns `success: true` empty zeros. - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-read.service.ts` (48–59) -> Action: Scope list to assignee/creator/admin; strip emails; bind hours-report to `req.user`.

 * **JWT-gated donation actor spoof + IDOR + public PII dumps.** `POST /api/donations` uses body `donor_id` and for `type==="money"` increments that user’s `total_donations_amount`. `PUT`/`DELETE /api/donations/:id` have no owner/admin check. Unauthenticated `GET /api/donations` `SELECT d.*` (577–661; `status` query overrides active-only default). Unauthenticated `GET :id` returns `donor_phone` + `donor_email` (667–696). Unauthenticated `GET user/:userId` returns `d.*` (782–804). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (478–534, 577–746, 782–804) -> Action: Bind `donor_id` to JWT; owner/admin on mutate; never return donor contact on public GET; drop `d.*` from list.

 * **JWT-gated post social-graph actor spoof.** `POST :postId/like` (1345–1406), comment create, comment `PUT` (1975–2026), comment `DELETE` (2054–2087), and comment-like (2152–2217) bind owner from body/query `user_id`, not `req.user`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` -> Action: Bind all social writes to `req.user.userId`.

 * **JWT-gated community-challenge privacy / tracker IDOR.** `GET /api/community-challenges` applies `COALESCE(c.is_public, true) = true` only when query `creator_id` is omitted (232–238). `GET :id` and `POST :id/join` skip `is_public`. `GET daily-tracker` uses query `user_id` instead of `CurrentUser()` (288–336). Contrast: `GET user/:userId/stats` and `GET :id/entries` correctly use `CurrentUser()`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-group-challenges.controller.ts` -> Action: Always filter `is_public` unless owner/admin; bind tracker subject to JWT.

 * **JWT-gated stats/details PII dump vs admin analytics.** `GET /api/stats/details/:statType` (834–981) dumps emails (`totalUsers` 862–876 LIMIT 500) and donor names/amounts to any JWT. Contrast: `GET /api/stats/analytics/users` is `AdminAuthGuard` (498–499). Unauthenticated `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` sums donation money. Unauthenticated `GET /api/stats/real-time` returns `users_online` + last-hour activity and uses `FULL OUTER JOIN rides ON 1=1` (803–809) — cartesian D×R, inflated counts + unauthenticated DoS. Unauthenticated `POST /api/stats/track-visit` (373–401) increments site_visits. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` -> Action: Admin-gate details; auth-gate analytics/real-time; replace `ON 1=1` with independent counts.

 * **Unauthenticated rides driver PII + write path + booking oversell.** No `UseGuards` in file. `GET /api/rides` selects `up.phone as driver_phone` (249). `GET :id` also selects `up.email as driver_email`. `GET user/:userId?type=passenger` returns `driver_phone`. `POST /api/rides` (47–225) trusts body `driver_id`; non-UUID values INSERT `user_profiles` at `${driver_id}@legacy.com` (80–118). `PUT :id` (695–736) updates title/seats/price/`status` of any ride. `DELETE :id` (738–755) cancels any ride. Book spoofs `passenger_id`; approve does not reject when capacity is exceeded (505–555). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` -> Action: Require JWT on all ride writes; bind driver/passenger to `req.user`; never INSERT `user_profiles` from an unauthenticated body; `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` and reject oversell; strip phone/email from public GET.

 * **Unauthenticated community-members `contact_info` dump.** No `UseGuards`. `GET /api/community-members` (186–245) and `GET :id` (247–273) `SELECT` `contact_info` (email/phone). `POST` / `PATCH` / `DELETE` remain unauthenticated writes. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-members.controller.ts` -> Action: Require `AdminAuthGuard`; never return `contact_info` on public GET.

 * **Unauthenticated collections `userId=all` JSONB dump.** No `UseGuards` on `ItemsController`. `GET /api/collections/:collection` calls `listAll` when `query.userId === "all"` (57–62). `listAll` (`items.service.ts` 227–277) runs `SELECT data FROM %I` with **no `user_id` predicate**. `ALLOWED_COLLECTIONS` includes `users`, `chats`, `messages`, `notifications`, `settings`. Unauthenticated POST/PUT/DELETE remain open. `GET :collection` is registered before static `GET popular-collections` / `GET cache-stats`, so those helpers return 400 Invalid collection. - File: `apps/api/src/items/items.controller.ts` (43–87); `apps/api/src/items/items.service.ts` (15–47, 227–277) -> Action: Require JWT; bind CRUD to `req.user.userId`; delete or admin-gate `userId=all`; reorder static routes before `:collection`.

 * **Unauthenticated CRM `SELECT *` contact dump.** No `UseGuards`. `GET /api/crm` (106–147) runs `SELECT * FROM crm_contacts`. `POST` / `PATCH` / `DELETE` remain unauthenticated (149–267). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts` -> Action: Require `AdminAuthGuard`; bind `created_by` to JWT on writes.

 * **Unauthenticated hidden-post bypass on user-posts, by-id, and comments (2026-09-06).** The public feed applies `(p.status IS NULL OR p.status != 'hidden')` (`posts.controller.ts` 754). Owner hide (`POST /api/posts/:postId/hide`, JWT + owner, 2434–2480) sets `status = 'hidden'`. `GET /api/posts/user/:userId` uses `WHERE p.author_id = $1` only (1159–1161) and still embeds `ride_data` / task / metadata. `GET /api/posts/:postId` uses `WHERE p.id = $1` only (1944–1945). `GET :postId/comments` (1726–1772) ignores `posts.status`. Concrete trigger: hide a post then `GET /api/posts/user/<authorId>` still returns it. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (754 vs 1023–1167, 1726–1772, 1813–1955, 2434–2480) -> Action: Apply the hidden-status predicate on user-posts, by-id, likes, and comments unless the caller is the author or an admin.

 * **NEW 2026-09-07 — Hidden-post liker roster remains public.** `GET /api/posts/:postId/likes` (1463–1520) has no `UseGuards` and no join on `posts.status`. It returns liker `id` / `name` / `avatar_url` plus a total count for **any** `postId`, including rows the owner just hid. `GET :postId/likes/check/:userId` (1527–1546) is the same like-state oracle on a hidden post. SRS §2.5.2 documents the likes list as a public read, but §2.5.4 hide is the documented soft-delete — a hidden post must not keep exposing its social graph. Concrete trigger: owner calls `POST /api/posts/<id>/hide`; `GET /api/posts` omits the row; `GET /api/posts/<id>/likes` still lists everyone who liked it. Complements the 09-06 content bypass (user-posts / by-id / comments). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1463–1546 vs 754, 2434–2480) -> Action: 404 (or empty) likes/check/comments when `posts.status = 'hidden'` unless the caller is the author or an admin; bind `likes/check` to JWT.

 * **Fully unguarded write surfaces (items-delivery, dedicated-items, session logout-all, places proxy).** No `UseGuards` on those controllers. Items-delivery: `POST` trusts body `owner_id` (32–71); `PUT`/`DELETE :id` are unscoped (185–196); `POST :id/reserve` and `POST requests` create requests as any client `userId`; `POST :id/deliver` (241–258) sets `status='delivered'` with no owner check and may complete a request as body `userId`. `GET :id` (180) precedes `GET requests` (216) so the request list is unreachable. Dedicated-items: unauthenticated CRUD; `GET :id` (69) shadows `GET search` (166) and `GET category/:category` (147). Session `GET user/:userId`, `DELETE logout/:sessionId`, `DELETE logout-all/:userId` are unauthenticated. Places `/autocomplete`, `/place-details`, `/search-stats` proxy `GOOGLE_API_KEY`; `/place-details` interpolates `place_id` without `encodeURIComponent` (75). Redis-test (get/set/delete/keys) is registered only when `NODE_ENV !== production` (`app.module.ts` 122) — Railway `railway.toml.dev` uses `NODE_ENV=development`, so it is live on that service. Knowledge-link `POST /api/donations/knowledge/links` remains public; DELETE checks admin roles in-handler. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/dedicated-items.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–103) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard` (admin where appropriate); bind owners to JWT; encode `place_id`; disable redis-test and session demo in deployed environments.

 * **JWT-gated task log-hours spoof.** `POST /api/tasks/:id/log-hours` UPSERTs as body `user_id` (384–445) with no `req.user` check. - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (384–445) -> Action: Bind `user_id` to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Admin-files list ACL too weak.** `GET /api/admin-files` and `GET folders` are `JwtAuthGuard` only; POST/DELETE correctly use `AdminAuthGuard`. Any authenticated user can list Finance/HR file URLs. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/admin-files.controller.ts` (80–140 vs 142–203) -> Action: Put list/folders behind `AdminAuthGuard`.

 * **Request-path `DROP TABLE ... CASCADE`.** `ensurePostsTable` (invoked from GET/POST post routes) can `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` when legacy columns are detected (78), plus likes/comments drops (309, 373–374). `tasks-schema.service.ts` (41) can drop `posts` via task side-effects. `DatabaseInit` drops donations/rides on startup (568, 670) when it decides the schema is legacy. Concrete trigger: any traffic that hits `ensurePostsTable` while the live table is missing `id` or `author_id`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (78, 309, 373–374); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Never DROP on a request path; gate destructive DDL behind an explicit offline migration.

 * **Committed credentials, backups, and env fingerprints.** `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` line 34 commits a `JWT_SECRET`. `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md` documents a production JWT secret (L102 is a placeholder; L126 is a real-looking value). `main.ts` 149–218 fingerprints env using committed DB/Redis password/host **substrings** (do not copy values). 49 tracked JSON backups under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` include `user_profiles` keys (email/phone/`password_hash`). - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` (34); `apps/api/src/main.ts` (149–218); `apps/api/data-backups/` -> Action: Rotate all fingerprinted credentials; replace substring detection with an explicit `ENVIRONMENT` flag; purge backups from git history; move JWT secrets to the secret store.

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Unauthenticated posts feed treats query `user_id` as the viewer.** `GET /api/posts` has no guard. `feed_scope=friends` plus `user_id` filters to that user’s own posts and the people they follow (`posts.controller.ts` 776–782) and the same id drives `is_liked` (793–823). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/posts?feed_scope=friends&user_id=<victim>` enumerates the victim’s follow graph among recent authors and whether they liked each returned post. `GET /api/posts/:postId?viewer_id=` is the same like-state oracle. Recommendation: Require JWT; bind viewer exclusively to `req.user.userId`; delete or auth-gate the likes/check and `viewer_id` query.

 * **Ride booking approval oversell.** `PUT /api/rides/bookings/:bookingId/status` (505–555) sets `approved` then only flips the ride to `full` after the fact — it does not reject when `approved + requested > available_seats`. Concurrent books can oversell. Recommendation: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` and reject when remaining seats are insufficient.

 * **Task hours-report catch returns success.** `GET /api/tasks/hours-report/:managerId` catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros, hiding failures. Recommendation: Return `success: false` and a generic error.

 * **Items-delivery / dedicated-items static-route shadows.** `GET /api/items-delivery/:id` is declared before `GET requests`, so `GET /api/items-delivery/requests` hits `getItemById("requests")`. Dedicated-items `GET :id` shadows `GET search` and `GET category/:category`. Recommendation: Declare static routes before parameterized `:id`.

 * **Collections static-route shadow.** `GET /api/collections/:collection` precedes `GET popular-collections` and `GET cache-stats` (those return 400 Invalid collection). `GET user-activity/:userId` is 2-segment and still reachable. Recommendation: Move static helpers above `:collection`.

 * **ClosedRoute task-type / duplicate handling.** `apps/mobile/bottomBarScreens/profile/ClosedRoute.tsx` still uses `require('../../utils/databaseService')` inside the component (36), `console.error` (316), and a loose `user?: any` prop. Duplicate-task skip (338) only compares `taskData.id` after mapping posts as `type === 'task'` — remaining type inconsistency can still double-count closed tasks vs task posts. Recommendation: Use the shared logger; unify closed-content type tags; drop the inline `require`.

 * **Client-facing `error.message` leaks.** Posts catch blocks (~1168–1182, ~1704–1716), tasks, dedicated-items, items.controller, and JwtAuthGuard Firebase verify (211–213) return raw `error.message` to clients. Recommendation: Map to generic errors; log details server-side only.

 * **Knowledge-link POST remains public.** `POST /api/donations/knowledge/links` accepts unauthenticated URLs (max 2048) and optional spoofed `createdByUserId`. DELETE correctly checks admin roles in-handler. Recommendation: JWT-gate create; ignore client `createdByUserId`.

 * **Places `place_id` is unencoded.** `GET /place-details` interpolates `place_id` into the Google URL without `encodeURIComponent` (75), enabling query-parameter injection against the Places API key. Recommendation: Encode the parameter; auth-gate the proxy.

---

## 🏗️ Architecture & Clean Code

 * **Clean Architecture target is unmet.** `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/layers-and-boundaries.md` requires Infrastructure → Application → Domain (inward). Scan of `apps/` and `packages/` found **zero** `/domain`, `/application`, or `/infrastructure` folders. Nest feature folders and RN screens/utils own SQL, HTTP, and policy in the same files. Closest pure islands remain `apps/api/src/tasks/task-completion-participants.ts`, `tasks-validation.ts`, `tasks-query-params.ts`, `tasks.types.ts` (no Nest/pg imports). `packages/` still only `config-eslint`.

 * **Privilege checks are inconsistent inside the same controller (authorization-in-one-file smell).** `PostsController` hides on the public feed only (754) and skips hide on user-posts / by-id / comments / **likes**. `StatsController` admin-gates `analytics/users` but JWT-only-gates `details/:statType`. `CommunityGroupChallengesController` binds `CurrentUser()` on stats/entries but query `user_id` on daily-tracker. `DonationsController` JWT-gates writes but binds `donor_id` from the body. `RidesController` has no guards at all.

 * **Fat HTTP/SQL files (LOC, apps+packages excluding node_modules/dist/spec/test).** `posts.controller.ts` (2741), `stats.controller.ts` (1709), `user-hierarchy.service.ts` (1675), `database.init.ts` (1374), `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (1097), `auth.controller.ts` (1029), `chat.controller.ts` (~813), `rides.controller.ts` (779). Mobile hubs: `bubbles_Data.tsx` (4852), `databaseService.ts` (1225), `SettingsScreen.tsx` (1220), `SecureApiService.ts` (1165) vs `apiService.ts` (1055). **197** source files exceed the ~200 LOC aim. `ItemsDeliveryService` is misplaced under `controllers/`.

 * **Positive contrasts (do not regress).** `ChallengesController` scopes CRUD to `CurrentUser()`. `NotificationsController.validateOwnership` binds URL `userId` to `req.user` (admins may read others). Knowledge contribution-request binds `req.user.userId`. Sync user/all/status use `AdminAuthGuard`. Post DELETE binds `getSessionUserId(req)` without a body fallback. Community-challenge join/entry creator binding and `GET user/:userId/stats` / `GET :id/entries` use `CurrentUser()`.

 * **SRS gaps still open** (`docs/SSOT/SRS/10-gaps-and-assumptions.md`): no `anonymity_level` column / filtering; no `/api/operator/*` module; `OperatorAuthGuard` exists but is unused; events/organizations schema without controllers; custom JWT instead of a battle-tested library.

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. If Admin SDK is initialized and no local row matches, `tryAutoCreateUserFromFirebase` calls `admin.auth().getUser(firebase_uid)` **without an ID token** and provisions a KC user + tokens from that UID. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body.

 * **Primary mobile login accepts email-only (password verification skipped, including empty string).** `POST /api/users/login` verifies argon2 **only when both** `loginData.password` and `user.password_hash` are truthy (223–231). Omitting `password`, sending `password: ""`, or targeting a null hash falls through to `success: true` and returns email, phone, roles, and `parent_manager_id`. `/auth/login` correctly rejects missing hash and invalid passwords. Combined with resolve-id, the documented mobile sequence is an email-only takeover. Contrasts SRS §2.1.2. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (116–118); `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (190–263, especially 223–231) -> Action: Always require a non-empty password; fail closed if hash is missing; never return `success: true` without `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated root-admin bootstrap via committed fallback email (identity-source split).** `KC_ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL_FALLBACK` is committed in `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/register` with that email sets `initialRoles = ["user", "admin"]` (101–102). Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/login` with the same email runs `array_append(roles, 'admin')` **before** the optional password check (212–220), then succeeds without a password. `getRootAdminEmail()` reads only `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` (empty if unset) and is used solely to skip `roles` writes. The PUT role/hierarchy **block** (281–297) compares a **hardcoded Gmail literal**, not the env var and not the shared constant. Setting `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` to a different mailbox does not stop bootstrap of the fallback address. - File: `apps/api/src/config/kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`; `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (101–102, 212–231); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (52–56, 281–297, 349–361) -> Action: Single source of truth for root-admin identity; require verified secret/env match plus password; never mutate roles before `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated register binds attacker-chosen Firebase UID.** `POST /api/users/register` has no guard. INSERT writes `userData.firebase_uid || userData.id || null` into `user_profiles.firebase_uid` (139). Password is optional (97–99). The victim’s later Firebase ID-token path attaches the attacker’s KC row. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (20–33, 97–99, 139) -> Action: Ignore client `firebase_uid`/`id` on public register; require a verified Firebase ID token before binding.

 * **JWT-gated profile mass-assignment + password IDOR (`PUT /api/users/:id`).** `UpdateUserBody` accepts `password`, `roles`, and `firebase_uid`. Controller uses `JwtAuthGuard` only — **no ownership check and no `req.user` binding**. Lookup matches UUID **or** email / `firebase_uid` / `google_id` (267). Root-admin block covers roles/hierarchy only; `password` and `firebase_uid` are still written (303–347). Any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) can reset any other user’s password or steal their Firebase identity. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 267, 281–361) -> Action: Bind `:id` to `req.user.userId` unless admin; strip `password`/`roles`/`firebase_uid` from the public DTO; extend the root-admin carve-out to password and identity fields.

 * **Unauthenticated user directory and profile PII.** `GET /api/users`, `GET /api/users/:id`, `GET /api/users/search`, `GET /api/users/stats/summary`, `GET /api/users/:id/activities`, and `GET /api/users/:id/stats` have no guards. `getUserById` / `searchUsers` return email, phone, and roles. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 101–123, 135–163) -> Action: Require auth (and minimize fields) on list/search/profile reads.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** JWT-gated promote/demote/set-manager/manage-hierarchy trust body `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. `setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` **skips the admin check** when the id is omitted or not found (495–504). `manageHierarchy` (871–914) applies add/remove with no JWT binding. Unauthenticated `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, and `GET :id/hierarchy` leak the org tree. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` (492–517, 871–914, 945–980) -> Action: Bind actor to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard`; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **JWT refresh copies stale roles (SRS §2.1.4 violation).** `refreshAccessToken` copies `payload.roles` from the refresh token and never reloads `user_profiles.roles`. Demoted admins keep elevated roles until the 30-day refresh token expires or is revoked. Redis existence **is** checked in `verifyToken`. HMAC compare uses `!==` not `timingSafeEqual` (308). Missing `exp` is treated as not-expired (`payload.exp < now` is false when `exp` is undefined). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/jwt.service.ts` (146–177, 117–121, 308) -> Action: Reload roles from DB on refresh; reject tokens without `exp`; use `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **Three additional unauthenticated login paths besides resolve-id.** (1) Firebase JWKS fallback accepts any Google-signed Firebase ID token unless `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID` is set (`firebase-admin.service.ts` 11–14, 177–181); fabricated `exp=now+3600` if claim missing (216–219). (2) `POST /auth/google` falls back to `GET oauth2/v3/userinfo` with a raw Bearer access token, hardcodes `emailVerified: true`, and skips `aud` binding (613–698). (3) JwtAuthGuard Firebase fallback (117–134) then looks up `user_profiles` by `firebase_uid`. Combined with register/profile `firebase_uid` mass-assignment this is account takeover. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/firebase-admin.service.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/auth.controller.ts` (613–698); `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (117–134, 170–213) -> Action: Require `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID` and reject tokens for other projects; accept Google **ID tokens** only with `aud` bound to this app; never mint a session from an access token.

 * **JwtAuthGuard shared HS256-header rate-limit bucket + unauthenticated plant-block.** `checkRateLimit(token.substring(0, 16), "api_access")` — issued header is always `{alg:HS256,typ:JWT}` so the first 16 chars are always `eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1`. 100 req/60s then 5-min block is **global** for every JWT user. Thrown as `UnauthorizedException` (401); mobile `authFetchInterceptor` clears the session on 401. Unauthenticated `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` deletes both the window and the block key. Unauthenticated `POST /rate-limit/custom` passes client `rule` + `customKey` into `applyCustomRateLimit` — two calls with `{requests:1, windowMs:1000, blockDurationMs:86400000}` and identifier `eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1` write `blocked:api_access:eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1` and 401 **every** JWT user (mobile logout). `POST /rate-limit/stress-test` accepts unbounded `delayMs` (HTTP timeout 30s at `main.ts` 441–443). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (96–114); `apps/api/src/auth/services/rate-limit.service.ts` (82–141, 181–192, 269–276); `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (61–97, 155, 233–264) -> Action: Key rate limits by `userId` (or a hash of the full token); never throw 401 for quota; delete or auth-gate `/rate-limit/*`; cap `delayMs`.

 * **ThrottlerModule is registered without a global `APP_GUARD`.** Zero `APP_GUARD` matches under `apps/api/src`. Only `AuthController` and `NotificationsController` opt in to `ThrottlerGuard`. Primary mobile `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` are unthrottled. Contrasts SRS §3.1 (global 60 req/60s). - File: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118) -> Action: Register `ThrottlerGuard` as `APP_GUARD`; explicitly throttle login/register.

 * **Unauthenticated chat write impersonation.** Class-level `OptionalAuthGuard` **always returns true** (`jwt-auth.guard.ts` 338). Unauthenticated `POST /messages` uses body `sender_id` (441–465). Authenticated mismatch is rejected (450–455); guests are not. Mark-read falls back to `body.user_id` (679, 759). Guest GET paths also read conversations/messages. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 267–268, 378–387, 441–465) -> Action: Require JWT on writes; bind `sender_id` exclusively to `req.user.userId`; do not serve conversation bodies to anonymous callers.

 * **Spoofable social graph and engagement actors.** Follow/unfollow use body `follower_id` not `req.user` (`user-follow.service.ts`). JWT-gated `POST /api/posts/:postId/like` uses body `user_id` only (1345–1406). Comment create (1573–1584), comment like (2152–2217), comment **PUT** (1975–2026), and comment **DELETE** (2054–2087) bind owner from body/query `user_id`, not `req.user`. Concrete trigger: any JWT + `PUT/DELETE .../comments/:id` with the victim’s `user_id`. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts`; `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1345–1406, 1573–1584, 1975–2087, 2152–2217) -> Action: Bind actor exclusively to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Donation create spoof + unscoped mutate + PII reads + list dump.** JWT-gated `POST /api/donations` uses body `donor_id` (never `req.user`) and for `type==="money"` increments that user’s `total_donations_amount` (478–534). JWT-gated `PUT`/`DELETE /api/donations/:id` have **no owner/admin check** (698–746). Unauthenticated `GET /api/donations/:id` returns `donor_phone` + `donor_email` (667–696). Unauthenticated `GET /api/donations/user/:userId` returns `d.*` history (782–804). Unauthenticated `GET /api/donations` runs `SELECT d.*` (595–661) — amount, `donor_id`, `metadata`, `location`, images — for every matching row (default `status=active`; query `status` removes that filter). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/donations?limit=50&offset=0` then paginate; `GET /api/donations?status=completed` for non-active rows. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (478–534, 577–661, 667–804) -> Action: Bind `donor_id` to JWT; require owner/admin on PUT/DELETE; never return phone/email on public GET; project list fields (no `d.*`); auth-gate per-user history.

 * **JWT-gated stats detail dump (`GET /api/stats/details/:statType`).** Method is `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)` only (834–836). `statType=totalUsers` runs `SELECT name, email, city, join_date, created_at FROM user_profiles … LIMIT 500` (862–876). `statType=totalMoneyDonated` returns donor names and amounts (878–894). `statType=uniqueDonors` / `recurringDonationsAmount` return recurring donor names and amounts (929–947). Results are Redis-cached for 5 minutes under `stat_details_${statType}` (shared across callers). Concrete trigger: any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) + `GET /api/stats/details/totalUsers`. Contrast in the same controller: `GET /api/stats/analytics/users` is `AdminAuthGuard` (498–499). SRS §2.10.1 lists dashboard as admin stats; this detail endpoint is not in the public community-stats contract. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (834–981 vs 498–499) -> Action: Put `details/:statType` behind `AdminAuthGuard`; never return emails or donor amounts to a generic JWT; do not share one cache key across privilege levels.

 * **Unauthenticated stats money totals + cartesian DoS.** `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` (donation money SUM) and `GET /api/stats/real-time` (`users_online` + last-hour activity) have no guards. Real-time donations/rides subquery is `FULL OUTER JOIN rides ON 1=1` (803–809) — cartesian D×R, inflated counts and an unauthenticated DoS as tables grow. `analytics/users` correctly uses `AdminAuthGuard`. Unauthenticated `POST /api/stats/track-visit` (373–401) increments `site_visits` and clears caches with no auth or throttle. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (373–401, 439–496, 782–831) -> Action: Guard both GET routes; replace `ON 1=1` with independent counts; throttle or drop public `track-visit`.

 * **JWT-gated task-by-id / tree / subtasks dump creator and assignee emails.** `GET /api/tasks` is already unscoped (74–146). `GET :id` (191–204), `GET :id/subtasks` (214–226), and `GET :id/tree` (237–249) are `JwtAuthGuard` only and never bind `req.user`. `TasksReadService.getTaskById` (48–59) and the matching subtask/tree SELECTs embed `creator_details` / `assignees_details` via `json_build_object('id', …, 'email', u.email, …)`. Concrete trigger: mint any JWT (including resolve-id) + `GET /api/tasks/<uuid>` returns title, description, checklist, hours, and emails. Complements hours-report IDOR (568–587). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (74–146, 191–249, 568–587); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-read.service.ts` (48–59, 82–91, 134–140) -> Action: Scope list/get/tree to assignee, creator, or admin; strip emails from non-admin projections.

 * **JWT-gated `GET /api/tasks/hours-report/:managerId` has no owner/admin check.** Returns org-tree names, user IDs, hours, and task titles (`tasks-read.service.ts` 151–311). Manager id resolves UUID or email/`firebase_uid`. Catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros (312–323 and controller 574–586). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (568–587); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-read.service.ts` (151–311) -> Action: Bind `:managerId` to `req.user` (or require admin); fail closed on errors.

 * **JWT-gated `POST /api/tasks/:id/log-hours` UPSERTs as body `user_id`.** Never reads `req.user` (384–445). Any JWT can attribute hours to another user. - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (384–445) -> Action: Bind `user_id` to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Community-challenge private-list leak + daily-tracker IDOR.** Create persists `is_public` (57–92). List applies `AND COALESCE(c.is_public, true) = true` **only when query `creator_id` is omitted** (232–238). `GET :id` (496–546) and `POST :id/join` (574–590) skip `is_public`. JWT-gated `GET /api/community-challenges/daily-tracker` uses query `user_id` instead of `CurrentUser()` (288–336) and returns victim streaks/entries. Contrast: `getUserStatistics` uses `CurrentUser()` (846–848); `getChallengeEntries` uses `CurrentUser()` (805–812). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/users` (unauth) yields a victim UUID; any JWT then `GET /api/community-challenges?creator_id=<victim>` and `GET .../daily-tracker?user_id=<victim>`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (57–92, 232–238, 288–336, 496–546, 574–590 vs 805–848) -> Action: Always apply `is_public` unless caller is creator/admin; bind daily-tracker to `CurrentUser()`.

 * **Unauthenticated rides list/detail dump driver phone and email.** No `UseGuards` in `rides.controller.ts`. `GET /api/rides` (227–332) selects `up.phone as driver_phone` (249) plus `r.*` for every matching active future ride. `GET /api/rides/:id` (338–369) additionally selects `up.email as driver_email` (350). `GET /api/rides/user/:userId` is two-segment and reachable; when `type` is anything other than `driver` (or omitted, which takes the driver branch), the passenger query returns `up.phone as driver_phone` (637–644). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/rides` enumerates driver phones; `GET /api/rides/<uuid>` returns that driver’s phone **and** email; `GET /api/rides/user/<uuid>?type=passenger` dumps phones of drivers that user booked. Complements unauthenticated `POST :id/book` spoof of `passenger_id` (374–468) and `PUT bookings/:bookingId/status` approval with **no capacity reject** (505–555). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (227–369, 374–555, 579–654) -> Action: Strip phone/email from public projections; require JWT on book/approve/user-history; bind passenger to `req.user`; reject approve when remaining seats would go negative inside the same transaction.

 * **Unauthenticated ride write-path (create / update / cancel) plus auto-provision of `user_profiles`.** No `UseGuards`. `POST /api/rides` (47–225) trusts body `driver_id`. If that value is a UUID, the ride (and a matching `posts` row with `author_id = driverUuid`, 148–175) is attributed to that existing user. If it is **not** a UUID, the handler looks up `settings->>'legacy_id'` or `email = ${driver_id}@legacy.com` and, on miss, **INSERTs** `user_profiles (email, name, settings)` (98–118). `PUT /api/rides/:id` (695–736) applies `COALESCE` updates to title, description, times, `available_seats`, `price_per_seat`, requirements, and **`status`** with no owner check. `DELETE /api/rides/:id` (738–755) sets `status = 'cancelled'` for any id. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/rides` with `{ "driver_id":"audit-probe","from_location":{"name":"A"},"to_location":{"name":"B"},"departure_time":"2026-09-07T08:00:00Z" }` creates `audit-probe@legacy.com` and a public ride; `PUT /api/rides/<uuid>` with `{ "status":"cancelled","available_seats":0 }` silently mutates a victim ride; `DELETE /api/rides/<uuid>` cancels it. Complements the 2026-09-03 driver-phone/email dump and the already-listed book/approve oversell. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (47–225, 695–755) -> Action: Require JWT on POST/PUT/DELETE; bind `driver_id` to `req.user.userId`; never INSERT `user_profiles` from an unauthenticated body; require owner/admin on update/cancel.

 * **Unauthenticated community-members `contact_info` dump.** No `UseGuards`. `GET /api/community-members` (186–245) and `GET :id` (247–273) `SELECT` `contact_info` (typed as `{ email?, phone? }`). `POST` / `PATCH` / `DELETE` remain unauthenticated writes. Concrete trigger: `GET /api/community-members` returns every member’s email/phone JSON; `POST /api/community-members` with `{ "name":"x","role":"y","contact_info":{"email":"a","phone":"b"} }` persists attacker-controlled PII that the next GET exposes. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-members.controller.ts` (186–273, 294–439) -> Action: Require `AdminAuthGuard` (or JWT + field minimization); never return `contact_info` on public GET.

 * **Unauthenticated collections `userId=all` JSONB dump.** No `UseGuards` on `ItemsController`. `GET /api/collections/:collection` (43–78) calls `listAll` when `query.userId === "all"` (57–62) **or** when `userId` is omitted and `collection === "links"`. `listAll` (`items.service.ts` 227–277) runs `SELECT data FROM %I` with **no `user_id` predicate** after `pg-format` `%I` on an allowlisted table name. `ALLOWED_COLLECTIONS` (15–47) includes `users`, `chats`, `messages`, `notifications`, `settings`, `bookmarks`, `donations`, `tasks`, and other tenant-scoped stores. Concrete trigger: `GET /api/collections/messages?userId=all` (or `chats` / `notifications` / `users` / `settings`) returns every JSONB document in that table if the collection table is populated. Combined with unauthenticated `POST /api/collections/:collection` (80–87) an attacker can also write arbitrary JSONB and then dump it. Complements the already-listed collections write surface and the static-route shadow of `popular-collections` / `cache-stats`. - File: `apps/api/src/items/items.controller.ts` (43–87); `apps/api/src/items/items.service.ts` (15–47, 65–90, 227–277) -> Action: Require JWT; bind list/create/update/delete to `req.user.userId`; delete or admin-gate the `userId=all` / `listAll` path; never treat `users`/`chats`/`messages`/`notifications` as public collections.

 * **Unauthenticated CRM `SELECT *` contact dump.** No `UseGuards` on `CrmController`. `GET /api/crm` (106–147) runs `SELECT * FROM crm_contacts` (optionally filtered by `status` / `search`) and caches the full row set for 10 minutes. Columns include name, capabilities, desire, time_availability, source, referrer, status, and `created_by`. `POST` / `PATCH` / `DELETE` remain unauthenticated writes (149–267). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/crm` returns every CRM contact’s personal/capability record; `POST /api/crm` with `{ "name":"x","desire":"…","referrer":"…" }` persists attacker-controlled rows that the next GET exposes. Same elevation pattern as the community-members `contact_info` dump. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts` (106–267) -> Action: Require `AdminAuthGuard`; never return desire/referrer/capabilities on a public GET; bind `created_by` to JWT on writes.

 * **NEW 2026-09-06 — Unauthenticated hidden-post bypass on user-posts, by-id, and comments.** The public feed (`GET /api/posts`) applies `(p.status IS NULL OR p.status != 'hidden')` (`posts.controller.ts` 754). Owner hide (`POST /api/posts/:postId/hide`, JWT + owner check, 2434–2472) sets `status = 'hidden'` and is the documented soft-delete. Two unauthenticated read paths skip that predicate: `GET /api/posts/user/:userId` uses `WHERE p.author_id = $1` only (1159–1161) and still embeds `ride_data` (from/to, price, requirements, metadata), task assignees/hours, and `p.metadata`; `GET /api/posts/:postId` uses `WHERE p.id = $1` only (1944–1945). `GET /api/posts/:postId/comments` (1726–1772) selects comments by `c.post_id` with no join on `posts.status`. Concrete trigger: `GET /api/users` (unauth) yields an author UUID; after the owner hides a post, `GET /api/posts` omits it but `GET /api/posts/user/<authorId>` still returns the hidden row (and nested ride/task payload); `GET /api/posts/<postId>` and `GET /api/posts/<postId>/comments` remain readable. This is an authorization hole in the hide feature, not just a feed cosmetic. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (754 vs 1023–1167, 1726–1772, 1813–1955, 2434–2480) -> Action: Apply the same hidden-status predicate on user-posts, by-id, likes, and comments unless the caller is the author or an admin; treat hide as authorization, not feed-only filtering.

 * **Unauthenticated rides booking + approval oversell.** Covered with the rides driver-PII item above; book spoofs `passenger_id` (374–468); approve does not re-check capacity (505–555). Concurrent books can oversell. -> Action: Require JWT; bind passenger to `req.user`; `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` and reject when `approved + requested > available_seats`.

 * **Fully unguarded write surfaces (items-delivery, dedicated-items, session logout-all, places proxy).** No `UseGuards` on those controllers. Dedicated-items and items-delivery expose unauthenticated CRUD (create spoofs `owner_id`; reserve/deliver spoof `userId`). Session `GET user/:userId`, `DELETE logout/:sessionId`, `DELETE logout-all/:userId` are unauthenticated. Places `/autocomplete`, `/place-details`, `/search-stats` proxy `GOOGLE_API_KEY`; `/place-details` interpolates `place_id` without `encodeURIComponent` (75). Redis-test (get/set/delete/keys) is registered only when `NODE_ENV !== production` (`app.module.ts` 122) — Railway `railway.toml.dev` uses `NODE_ENV=development`, so it is live on that service. Collections and CRM reads are first-class items above; ride writes remain the 2026-09-05 first-class item. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/dedicated-items.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–103) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard` (admin where appropriate); bind owners to JWT; encode `place_id`; disable redis-test and session demo in deployed environments.

 * **Admin-files list ACL too weak.** `GET /api/admin-files` and `GET folders` are `JwtAuthGuard` only; POST/DELETE correctly use `AdminAuthGuard`. Any authenticated user can list Finance/HR file URLs. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/admin-files.controller.ts` (80–140 vs 142–203) -> Action: Put list/folders behind `AdminAuthGuard`.

 * **Request-path `DROP TABLE ... CASCADE`.** `ensurePostsTable` (invoked from GET/POST post routes) can `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` when legacy columns are detected (78), plus likes/comments drops (309, 373–374). `tasks-schema.service.ts` (41) can drop `posts` via task side-effects. `DatabaseInit` drops donations/rides on startup (568, 670) when it decides the schema is legacy. Concrete trigger: any traffic that hits `ensurePostsTable` while the live table is missing `id` or `author_id`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (67–80, 176–199, 842); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Never DROP on a request path; gate destructive DDL behind an explicit offline migration.

 * **Committed credentials, backups, and env fingerprints.** `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` line 34 commits a `JWT_SECRET`. `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md` documents a production JWT secret (L102 is a placeholder; L126 is a real-looking value). `main.ts` 149–218 fingerprints env using committed DB/Redis password/host **substrings** (do not copy values). 49 tracked JSON backups under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` include `user_profiles` keys (email/phone/`password_hash`). - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` (34); `apps/api/src/main.ts` (149–218); `apps/api/data-backups/` -> Action: Rotate all fingerprinted credentials; replace substring detection with an explicit `ENVIRONMENT` flag; purge backups from git history; move JWT secrets to the secret store.

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Unauthenticated posts feed treats query `user_id` as the viewer.** `GET /api/posts` has no guard. `feed_scope=friends` plus `user_id` filters to that user’s own posts and the people they follow (`posts.controller.ts` 776–782) and the same id drives `is_liked` (793–823). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/posts?feed_scope=friends&user_id=<victim>` enumerates the victim’s follow graph among recent authors and whether they liked each returned post. Default unscoped feed is already public, so this is a social-graph / like-state leak rather than extra post content. `GET /api/posts/:postId/likes/check/:userId` (1527–1546) and `GET /api/posts/:postId?viewer_id=` (1816–1948) are the same like-state oracle without auth. Recommendation: Require JWT; bind viewer exclusively to `req.user.userId`; delete or auth-gate the likes/check and `viewer_id` query.

 * **Dedicated-items and items-delivery static routes shadowed by `GET :id`.** `ItemsDeliveryController` registers `GET :id` (180) before `GET requests` (216), so `GET /api/items-delivery/requests` is handled as `id="requests"`. `DedicatedItemsController` registers `GET :id` (69) before `GET search` (166) **and** `GET category/:category` (147), so `GET /api/dedicated-items/search` and `GET /api/dedicated-items/category/<slug>` are unreachable (category hits `getById("category")`). Recommendation: Move all static and multi-segment routes above parameterized `:id`.

 * **Collections helper routes shadowed.** `ItemsController` `GET :collection` (43) is registered before static `GET popular-collections` (136) and `GET cache-stats` (153), so those helpers return 400 Invalid collection. `GET user-activity/:userId` is two-segment and still reachable. Recommendation: Register static paths first.

 * **Hours-report and several catch blocks lie about success.** Hours-report catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros. Posts/tasks/dedicated-items/items catch blocks leak `error.message` to clients. JwtAuthGuard `verifyFirebaseToken` also returns `error.message` (211–213). Recommendation: Fail closed; map to stable error codes; never return raw driver messages.

 * **Ride approval oversell / booking race.** Seat check uses remaining seats at book time for pending rows, but approval does not re-check capacity before commit. Two pending bookings that together exceed seats can both be approved. Recommendation: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the ride row and reject when `approved + requested > available_seats`.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang.** `authFetchInterceptor` concurrent 401s subscribe to `refreshSubscribers`. On refresh failure the leader sets `isRefreshing = false` and **clears the array without calling subscribers** (75–81), so waiters never resolve. Recommendation: Always invoke waiters (with a failure token) or reject their promises in `finally`.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** Posts-derived closed tasks are filtered with `c.type === 'task'` (324), but the `getTasks` fallback pushes `type: 'task_post'` (351). Dedup therefore misses the fallback rows and UI filters keyed on one type miss the other. Recommendation: Normalize to a single type before push.

 * **Knowledge-link POST remains public; DELETE checks admin roles in-handler.** `POST /api/donations/knowledge/links` has no guard (227–301) and writes caller-supplied `createdByUserId` / `displayName`. DELETE (305–318) correctly checks admin roles on `req.user` after `JwtAuthGuard`. Recommendation: Rate-limit and optionally auth-gate public writes; keep admin delete.

 * **Positive contrasts (do not “fix”).** `ChallengesController` scopes CRUD to `CurrentUser()`. `NotificationsController.validateOwnership` binds URL `userId` to `req.user` (admins may override). Community-challenge join/entry and `GET user/:userId/stats` / `GET :id/entries` bind JWT. `/auth/login` verifies argon2. Admin-tables is class-level `AdminAuthGuard`. Admin-files POST/DELETE use `AdminAuthGuard`. Knowledge contribution-request binds `req.user.userId`. Sync user/all/status use `AdminAuthGuard`. Post **delete** binds `getSessionUserId(req)` (2618–2672) without a body `user_id` fallback — contrast the hide/update paths, which still fall back to `body.user_id` after `req.user`.

## 🏗️ Architecture & Clean Code

 * **No Clean Architecture layers in apps/ or packages/.** Target in `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/layers-and-boundaries.md` is Infrastructure → Application → Domain (inward). Scan found **zero** `/domain`, `/application`, or `/infrastructure` folders. Domain logic lives inside Nest controllers/services that import `pg` `Pool` and Redis. Closest pure islands: `apps/api/src/tasks/task-completion-participants.ts`, `tasks-validation.ts`, `tasks-query-params.ts`, `tasks.types.ts` (no Nest/pg imports).

 * **Fat HTTP + SQL controllers (same product HEAD).** `posts.controller.ts` (2741), `stats.controller.ts` (1709), `user-hierarchy.service.ts` (1675), `database.init.ts` (1374), `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (1097), `donations.controller.ts` (892), `chat.controller.ts` (813), `rides.controller.ts` (779). `items-delivery.service.ts` (748) is misplaced under `controllers/`. Users controller is thinner but services still inject `Pool`.

 * **SRS anonymity / operator matching still unimplemented (gap, not a new regression).** No `anonymity_level` symbol under `apps/api/src`. Logged in `docs/SSOT/SRS/10-gaps-and-assumptions.md` (§10.1): column missing, no feed filtering, no `OperatorMatchingModule`. Do not treat as a newly introduced vuln — it is an acknowledged missing feature vs §2.5.7 / §3.2.

 * **Privilege-check inconsistency in one file is the architectural smell behind several vulns.** `stats.controller.ts` puts `analytics/users` behind `AdminAuthGuard` and `details/:statType` behind JWT-only. `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` binds `CurrentUser()` on stats/entries but query `user_id` on daily-tracker. `donations.controller.ts` JWT-gates writes then ignores `req.user`. `rides.controller.ts` has no guard at all: it joins `user_profiles.phone`/`email` onto public list/detail **and** accepts unauthenticated POST/PUT/DELETE (including `user_profiles` INSERT for non-UUID `driver_id`). `ItemsController` treat `userId=all` as a public dump of otherwise tenant-scoped collections. `PostsController` applies hidden-status on the feed only and skips it on user-posts / by-id / comments. Fix pattern: one ownership helper per resource, used on every route; project PII only for authorized viewers.

 * **Mobile debt hubs (unchanged).** `utils/databaseService.ts` (1225), `apiService.ts` (1055), `enhancedDatabaseService.ts` (897), duplicate `google_auth/SecureApiService.ts` (1165). Largest tracked source: `apps/mobile/utils/bubbles_Data.tsx` (4852). `packages/` still only `config-eslint`.
