## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **NEW 2026-09-01 — Unauthenticated posts feed treats query `user_id` as the viewer.** `GET /api/posts` has no guard. `feed_scope=friends` plus `user_id` filters to that user’s own posts and the people they follow (`posts.controller.ts` 776–782) and the same id drives `is_liked` (793–823). Concrete trigger: `GET /api/posts?feed_scope=friends&user_id=<victim>` enumerates the victim’s follow graph among recent authors and whether they liked each returned post. Default unscoped feed is already public, so this is a social-graph / like-state leak rather than extra post content. `GET /api/posts/:postId/likes/check/:userId` (1527–1546) is the same like-state oracle without auth. Recommendation: Require JWT; bind viewer exclusively to `req.user.userId`; delete or auth-gate the likes/check route.

 * **Dedicated-items and items-delivery static routes shadowed by `GET :id`.** `ItemsDeliveryController` registers `GET :id` (180) before `GET requests` (216), so `GET /api/items-delivery/requests` is handled as `id="requests"`. `DedicatedItemsController` registers `GET :id` (69) before `GET search` (166) **and** `GET category/:category` (147), so `GET /api/dedicated-items/search` and `GET /api/dedicated-items/category/<slug>` are unreachable (category hits `getById("category")`). Recommendation: Move all static and multi-segment routes above parameterized `:id`.

 * **Collections helper routes shadowed.** `ItemsController` `GET :collection` (43) is registered before static `GET popular-collections` (136) and `GET cache-stats` (153), so those helpers return 400 Invalid collection. `GET user-activity/:userId` is two-segment and still reachable. Recommendation: Register static paths first.

 * **Hours-report and several catch blocks lie about success.** Hours-report catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros. Posts/tasks/dedicated-items/items catch blocks leak `error.message` to clients. JwtAuthGuard `verifyFirebaseToken` also returns `error.message` (211–213). Recommendation: Fail closed; map to stable error codes; never return raw driver messages.

 * **Ride approval oversell / booking race.** Seat check uses remaining seats at book time for pending rows, but approval does not re-check capacity before commit. Two pending bookings that together exceed seats can both be approved. Recommendation: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the ride row and reject when `approved + requested > available_seats`.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang.** `authFetchInterceptor` concurrent 401s subscribe to `refreshSubscribers`. On refresh failure the leader sets `isRefreshing = false` and **clears the array without calling subscribers** (75–81), so waiters never resolve. Recommendation: Always invoke waiters (with a failure token) or reject their promises in `finally`.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** Posts-derived closed tasks use `type: 'task'`; `getTasks` fallbacks push `type: 'task_post'` (351). Dedup filters `c.type === 'task'` (324), so the two sources do not share a type and UI filters keyed on one miss the other. Recommendation: Normalize to a single type before push.

 * **Task done PATCH can duplicate completion posts.** Side-effects on mark-done are not idempotent across retries. Recommendation: Unique constraint / existence check on `(task_id, post_type=task_completion)` before insert.

 * **Sync API key is optional when left at the committed default.** `SYNC_API_KEY` defaults to `change-me-in-production`; `checkApiKey` runs only when the value is **not** that default (422–424, 511–513). Endpoints also have `AdminAuthGuard`, so this is defense-in-depth only — still fail-open if the guard is removed. Recommendation: Require a non-default key always; fail startup if unset.

 * **Community-challenge `GET user/:userId/stats` ignores the path param.** Handler binds only `CurrentUser()` (846–848), so `GET /api/community-challenges/user/<other>/stats` returns the caller’s stats, not the URL user’s. Misleading contract; not an IDOR. Recommendation: Either 403 when path ≠ session or drop `:userId` from the route.

---

## 🏗️ Architecture & Clean Code

 * **Clean Architecture target is unmet.** `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/layers-and-boundaries.md` requires Infrastructure → Application → Domain. There are **zero** `/domain`, `/application`, or `/infrastructure` folders under `apps/` or `packages/`. Nest feature folders own SQL + HTTP + policy in the same files. Closest pure islands: `apps/api/src/tasks/task-completion-participants.ts`, `tasks-validation.ts`, `tasks-query-params.ts`, `tasks.types.ts` (no Nest/pg imports). `packages/` only has `config-eslint`.

 * **Fat controllers still own DDL and business rules.** Worst API surfaces (LOC): `posts.controller.ts` (2741), `stats.controller.ts` (1709), `user-hierarchy.service.ts` (1675), `database.init.ts` (1374), `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (1097), `donations.controller.ts` (892), `chat.controller.ts` (813), `rides.controller.ts` (779). `items-delivery.service.ts` is misplaced under `controllers/`. Positive contrasts: `NotificationsController.validateOwnership`; `ChallengesController` + community join/entry bind `CurrentUser()` — **except** `daily-tracker` (query `user_id`) and list (`creator_id` bypasses `is_public`). Stats `details/:statType` is JWT-only while `analytics/users` is admin — same privilege split failure as hours-report / daily-tracker.

 * **Mobile debt hubs.** `utils/databaseService.ts` (1225), `apiService.ts` (1055), `enhancedDatabaseService.ts` (897); duplicate `google_auth/SecureApiService.ts` (1165). Duplicate mappers `mapPostToFeedItemForItemsScreen` / `ForTrumpScreen` vs `useFeedData` (already in tech-debt-log follow-ups). Largest tracked source: `apps/mobile/utils/bubbles_Data.tsx` (4852). **196** source `.ts`/`.tsx` files >200 LOC (apps+packages, excluding node_modules/dist/spec/test).

 * **SRS / code gaps unchanged** (`docs/SSOT/SRS/10-gaps-and-assumptions.md`): no `/api/operator/*` module; no `anonymity_level` column or server-side redaction; no matching tables; `OptionalAuthGuard` semantics conflict with “enrich but do not authorize writes.”

# 🛡️ Daily Audit Report: 2026-08-31

**Product HEAD audited:** `3d92f9d9cfbf7fa4e02b763eeb62a131d494f040` (merge #101 — identical to `origin/dev` and to the 2026-08-20 through 2026-08-30 audits)
**Audit branch:** `cursor/daily-repository-audit-d449`
**Scope:** Holistic security / bug / architecture / debt re-verification; secret, DDL, auth-guard, XSS/SQLi, npm, unguarded-controller, login-throttle, login-password-skip (including falsy empty-string), register identity-binding, root-admin bootstrap (including identity-source split), stats-surface, JWT-refresh-role, chat write-path, Firebase JWKS audience, JwtAuthGuard rate-limit identifier, Google OAuth access-token fallback, profile ownership/password IDOR (including root-admin carve-out), committed credential fingerprints, admin-files ACL, collections static-route shadowing, rate-limit custom-rule / stress-test, donation actor-binding / donation IDOR / donation-detail PII, task log-hours spoof, task hours-report IDOR, items-delivery + dedicated-items static-route shadowing, community-challenges daily-tracker IDOR, and **community-challenges private-list leak via `creator_id` / comment update-delete actor spoof**
**SRS mapping:** NFR §3.1–3.2 (`docs/SSOT/SRS/03-non-functional-requirements.md`); auth §2.1, users §2.2, donations §2.3, rides §2.4, posts §2.5, challenges §2.8, chat §2.7 (`docs/SSOT/SRS/functional/*`); backend §5; gaps §10
**Mode:** Report-only (no product code fixes in this pass — blast radius of confirmed issues is multi-module; prior daily-audit PRs #105–#146 remain open drafts and unmerged to `dev`)
**Delta vs 2026-08-30:** No product commits. All previously confirmed critical items re-verified at the same line numbers. New first-class findings: (1) JWT-gated `GET /api/community-challenges` skips the `is_public` filter whenever query `creator_id` is present, so any JWT can list another user’s private challenges (create already honors `dto.is_public`; `GET :id` and `POST :id/join` also omit an `is_public` check, so leaked IDs are immediately readable and joinable); (2) JWT-gated `PUT`/`DELETE /api/posts/:postId/comments/:commentId` bind the owner from body/query `user_id` and never from `req.user`, so any JWT can edit or delete another user’s comment by supplying that author’s id. npm audit totals again non-deterministic (**44 → 93 → 94**; critical still **4**: `handlebars`, `shell-quote`, `tar`, `websocket-driver`). Files >200 LOC: **195** (same product tree; prior reports counted 196 under a slightly looser exclude). Request-path XSS/SQLi patterns remain absent; rides/items-delivery `ORDER BY` columns are allowlisted.

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. If Admin SDK is initialized and no local row matches, `tryAutoCreateUserFromFirebase` calls `admin.auth().getUser(firebase_uid)` **without an ID token** and provisions a KC user + tokens from that UID. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body.

 * **Primary mobile login accepts email-only (password verification skipped, including empty string).** `POST /api/users/login` verifies argon2 **only when both** `loginData.password` and `user.password_hash` are truthy (223–231). Omitting `password`, sending `password: ""`, or targeting a null hash falls through to `success: true` and returns email, phone, roles, and `parent_manager_id`. `/auth/login` correctly rejects missing hash and invalid passwords. Combined with resolve-id, the documented mobile sequence is an email-only takeover. Contrasts SRS §2.1.2. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (116–118); `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (190–263, especially 223–231) -> Action: Always require a non-empty password; fail closed if hash is missing; never return `success: true` without `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated root-admin bootstrap via committed fallback email (identity-source split).** `KC_ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL_FALLBACK` is committed in `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/register` with that email sets `initialRoles = ["user", "admin"]` (101–102). Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/login` with the same email runs `array_append(roles, 'admin')` **before** the optional password check (212–220), then succeeds without a password. `getRootAdminEmail()` reads only `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` (empty if unset) and is used solely to skip `roles` writes. The PUT role/hierarchy **block** (281–297) compares a **hardcoded Gmail literal**, not the env var and not the shared constant. Setting `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` to a different mailbox does not stop bootstrap of the fallback address. - File: `apps/api/src/config/kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`; `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (101–102, 212–231); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (52–56, 281–297, 349–361) -> Action: Single source of truth for root-admin identity; require verified secret/env match plus password; never mutate roles before `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated register binds attacker-chosen Firebase UID.** `POST /api/users/register` has no guard. INSERT writes `userData.firebase_uid || userData.id || null` into `user_profiles.firebase_uid` (139). Password is optional (97–99). The victim’s later Firebase ID-token path attaches the attacker’s KC row. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (20–33, 97–99, 139) -> Action: Ignore client `firebase_uid`/`id` on public register; require a verified Firebase ID token before binding.

 * **JWT-gated profile mass-assignment + password IDOR (`PUT /api/users/:id`).** `UpdateUserBody` accepts `password`, `roles`, and `firebase_uid`. Controller uses `JwtAuthGuard` only — **no ownership check and no `req.user` binding**. Lookup matches UUID **or** email / `firebase_uid` / `google_id` (267). Root-admin block covers roles/hierarchy only; `password` and `firebase_uid` are still written (303–347). Any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) can reset any other user’s password or steal their Firebase identity. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 267, 281–361) -> Action: Bind `:id` to `req.user.userId` unless admin; strip `password`/`roles`/`firebase_uid` from the public DTO; extend the root-admin carve-out to password and identity fields.

 * **Unauthenticated user directory and profile PII.** `GET /api/users`, `GET /api/users/:id`, `GET /api/users/search`, `GET /api/users/stats/summary`, `GET /api/users/:id/activities`, and `GET /api/users/:id/stats` have no guards. `getUserById` / `searchUsers` return email, phone, and roles. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 101–123, 135–163) -> Action: Require auth (and minimize fields) on list/search/profile reads.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** JWT-gated promote/demote/set-manager/manage-hierarchy trust body `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. `setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` **skips the admin check** when the id is omitted or not found (495–504). `manageHierarchy` (871–914) applies add/remove with no JWT binding. Unauthenticated `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, and `GET :id/hierarchy` leak the org tree. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` (492–517, 871–914, 945–980) -> Action: Bind actor to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard`; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **JWT refresh copies stale roles (SRS §2.1.4 violation).** `refreshAccessToken` copies `payload.roles` from the refresh token and never reloads `user_profiles.roles`. Demoted admins keep elevated roles until the 30-day refresh token expires or is revoked. Redis existence **is** checked in `verifyToken`. HMAC compare uses `!==` not `timingSafeEqual` (308). Missing `exp` is treated as not-expired (`payload.exp < now` is false when `exp` is undefined). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/jwt.service.ts` (146–177, 117–121, 308) -> Action: Reload roles from DB on refresh; reject tokens without `exp`; use `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **Three additional unauthenticated login paths besides resolve-id.** (1) Firebase JWKS fallback accepts any Google-signed Firebase ID token unless `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID` is set (`firebase-admin.service.ts` 11–14, 177–181); fabricated `exp=now+3600` if claim missing (216–219). (2) `POST /auth/google` falls back to `GET oauth2/v3/userinfo` with a raw Bearer access token, hardcodes `emailVerified: true`, and skips `aud` binding (613–698). (3) JwtAuthGuard Firebase fallback (117–134) then looks up `user_profiles` by `firebase_uid`. Combined with register/profile `firebase_uid` mass-assignment this is account takeover. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/firebase-admin.service.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/auth.controller.ts` (613–698); `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (117–134, 170–213) -> Action: Require `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID`; reject access-token-only Google login; never fabricate `exp`.

 * **Shared HS256-header rate-limit bucket + unauthenticated plant-block.** `JwtAuthGuard` keys rate-limit as `token.substring(0, 16)`. Issued header is always `{alg:HS256,typ:JWT}`, so the first 16 chars are always `eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1`. 100 req/60s then 5-minute block is **global for all JWT users**, thrown as `UnauthorizedException` (401). Mobile `authFetchInterceptor` clears session on 401. Unauthenticated `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` deletes the shared window and block keys. Unauthenticated `POST /rate-limit/custom` plants `blocked:{customKey}:{identifier}` with attacker `blockDurationMs` — two calls with `{requests:1, windowMs:1000, blockDurationMs:86400000}` and `customKey=api_access` lock every JWT user for 24h. Unauthenticated `POST /rate-limit/stress-test` has unbounded `delayMs` (HTTP timeout 30s). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (96–114); `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (61–97, 155–184, 233–264) -> Action: Key by `userId` (after verify); register `APP_GUARD` ThrottlerGuard; delete or admin-guard the entire `RateLimitController`; cap `delayMs`.

 * **Primary mobile login/register unthrottled.** `ThrottlerModule` is configured (`app.module.ts` 72–77) but there is **zero** `APP_GUARD` under `apps/api/src`. Only `AuthController` and `NotificationsController` opt in. `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` (the mobile path) are unthrottled. Contrasts SRS §3.1. - File: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118) -> Action: Add global `APP_GUARD` ThrottlerGuard; decorate users login/register with a stricter `@Throttle`.

 * **Unauthenticated chat write impersonation.** Class-level `OptionalAuthGuard` always returns `true` (`jwt-auth.guard.ts` 338). Unauthenticated `POST /api/chat/messages` uses body `sender_id` (441–465). Authenticated mismatch is rejected (450–455); guests are not. Mark-read falls back to `body.user_id` (679, 759). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 441–465, 679, 759) -> Action: Require JWT on writes; bind `sender_id` to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Spoofable social-graph and feed actors (JWT present, body wins).** Follow/unfollow use body `follower_id` not `req.user`. Post like (1345–1406), comment create (1573–1584), and comment-like (2152–2217) use body `user_id` only. Hide/update/unhide fall back to `body.user_id` when session fields are missing (2301–2305). - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts` (19–34); `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1345–1406, 1573–1584, 2152–2217, 2283–2332) -> Action: Bind actor exclusively to `req.user.userId`.

 * **NEW 2026-08-31 — Comment update/delete IDOR.** JWT-gated `PUT /api/posts/:postId/comments/:commentId` takes ownership from body `user_id` (1985–2014) and never reads `req.user`. JWT-gated `DELETE` of the same resource takes ownership from query `user_id` (2054–2087) and likewise never binds the session. Concrete trigger: any JWT + `DELETE /api/posts/<postId>/comments/<commentId>?user_id=<comment_author>` deletes that comment; `PUT` with `{ "user_id": "<author>", "text": "..." }` overwrites it. Same controller already has this class of bug on create/like; update/delete are write-destructive and were not listed as first-class yesterday. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1975–2087) -> Action: Ignore client `user_id`; compare `post_comments.user_id` to `req.user.userId` (admin override only).

 * **Donation actor spoof, IDOR, and detail PII.** JWT-gated `POST /api/donations` uses body `donor_id` (never `req.user`) and for `type==="money"` increments that user’s `total_donations_amount` (478–534). JWT-gated `PUT`/`DELETE /api/donations/:id` have no owner/admin check (698–746). Unauthenticated `GET /api/donations/:id` returns `donor_phone` + `donor_email` (667–696). Knowledge-link POST remains public; DELETE correctly checks admin roles in-handler. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (478–534, 667–746, 233–301, 312–318) -> Action: Bind `donor_id` to JWT; owner-or-admin on mutate; redact donor contact on public GET; guard knowledge POST.

 * **Task hours spoof + hours-report IDOR.** JWT-gated `POST /api/tasks/:id/log-hours` UPSERTs as body `user_id` (384–445). JWT-gated `GET /api/tasks/hours-report/:managerId` has **no owner/admin check** (`tasks.controller.ts` 568–587; `tasks-read.service.ts` 151–311) and returns org-tree names, user IDs, hours, and task titles. Manager id resolves UUID or email/`firebase_uid`. Catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros (312–323 and controller 574–586), hiding failures. JWT-gated `GET /api/tasks` is an unscoped org-wide list (74–146). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (74–146, 384–445, 568–587); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-read.service.ts` (151–323) -> Action: Bind log-hours and report to `req.user`; scope list to assignee/admin; fail closed on report errors.

 * **Community daily-tracker IDOR (confirmed 2026-08-30).** Class is `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)`, but `GET /api/community-challenges/daily-tracker` reads `@Query("user_id")` and returns that user’s DAILY challenge rows, `current_streak` / `best_streak` / `total_entries` / `last_entry_date`, plus `entries_by_date`. Same controller already binds JWT correctly on `getUserStatistics` (`CurrentUser()`, 846–848) and `getChallengeEntries` (805–812). Concrete trigger: any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) + `GET /api/community-challenges/daily-tracker?user_id=<victim>`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (288–336 vs 805–812 / 846–848) -> Action: Ignore query `user_id`; bind to `CurrentUser().userId` (admin override only).

 * **NEW 2026-08-31 — Private community-challenge enumeration via `creator_id`.** Create persists `is_public` from the DTO (`isPublic = dto.is_public !== false`, lines 57–92). List applies `AND COALESCE(c.is_public, true) = true` **only in the else branch** when query `creator_id` is omitted (232–238). `GetChallengesFilterDto.creator_id` is a public query field. Concrete trigger: unauthenticated `GET /api/users` yields a victim UUID; any JWT then `GET /api/community-challenges?creator_id=<victim>` returns that user’s private challenges (title, description, goal, category, image). Related: `GET :id` (496–546) has no `is_public` check and returns top-10 participants (names, avatars, streaks); `POST :id/join` (574–590) checks existence + `is_active` only, so a leaked private id is immediately joinable. Contrast: create/update/delete correctly bind `CurrentUser()` as creator. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (57–92, 232–238, 496–546, 574–590); `apps/api/src/controllers/dto/community-challenge.dto.ts` (`creator_id` on `GetChallengesFilterDto`) -> Action: Always apply the `is_public` predicate unless the caller is the creator or an admin; reject join on private challenges except by invite/owner.

 * **Unauthenticated rides booking + approval oversell.** No `UseGuards` in `rides.controller.ts`. `POST :id/book` spoofs `passenger_id` (374–468). `PUT bookings/:bookingId/status` applies `status=approved` with **no capacity reject** — remaining_seats ≤ 0 only flips the ride to `full` after the fact (505–555). Concurrent books can oversell. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (374–555) -> Action: Require JWT; bind passenger to `req.user`; reject approve when remaining seats would go negative inside the same transaction.

 * **Fully unguarded write surfaces (CRM, community-members, items-delivery, dedicated-items, collections, session logout-all, places proxy).** No `UseGuards` on those controllers. Collections `POST/PUT/DELETE` write arbitrary allowlisted JSONB. Dedicated-items and items-delivery expose unauthenticated CRUD (create spoofs `owner_id`). Session `GET user/:userId`, `DELETE logout/:sessionId`, `DELETE logout-all/:userId` are unauthenticated. Places `/autocomplete`, `/place-details`, `/search-stats` proxy `GOOGLE_API_KEY`; `/place-details` interpolates `place_id` without `encodeURIComponent` (75). Redis-test (get/set/delete/keys) is registered only when `NODE_ENV !== production` (`app.module.ts` 122) — Railway `railway.toml.dev` uses `NODE_ENV=development`, so it is live on that service. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts`; `community-members.controller.ts`; `items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/{items,dedicated-items}.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–103) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard` (admin where appropriate); bind owners to JWT; encode `place_id`; disable redis-test and session demo in deployed environments.

 * **Admin-files list ACL too weak.** `GET /api/admin-files` and `GET folders` are `JwtAuthGuard` only; POST/DELETE correctly use `AdminAuthGuard`. Any authenticated user can list Finance/HR file URLs. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/admin-files.controller.ts` (80–140 vs 142–203) -> Action: Put list/folders behind `AdminAuthGuard`.

 * **Unauthenticated stats money totals + cartesian DoS.** `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` (donation money SUM) and `GET /api/stats/real-time` (`users_online` + last-hour activity) have no guards. Real-time donations/rides subquery is `FULL OUTER JOIN rides ON 1=1` (803–809) — cartesian D×R, inflated counts and an unauthenticated DoS as tables grow. `analytics/users` correctly uses `AdminAuthGuard`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (439–496, 782–831) -> Action: Guard both routes; replace `ON 1=1` with independent counts.

 * **Request-path `DROP TABLE ... CASCADE`.** `ensurePostsTable` (invoked from GET/POST post routes) can `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` when legacy columns are detected (78), plus likes/comments drops (309, 373–374). `tasks-schema.service.ts` (41) can drop `posts` via task side-effects. `DatabaseInit` drops donations/rides on startup (568, 670) when it decides the schema is legacy. Concrete trigger: any traffic that hits `ensurePostsTable` while the live table is missing `id` or `author_id`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (67–80, 176–199, 842); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Never DROP on a request path; gate destructive DDL behind an explicit offline migration.

 * **Committed credentials, backups, and env fingerprints.** `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` line 34 commits a `JWT_SECRET`. `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md` documents a production JWT secret (L102 is a placeholder; L126 is a real-looking value). `main.ts` 149–218 fingerprints env using committed DB/Redis password/host **substrings** (do not copy values). 49 tracked JSON backups under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` include `user_profiles` keys (email/phone/`password_hash`). - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` (34); `apps/api/src/main.ts` (149–218); `apps/api/data-backups/` -> Action: Rotate all fingerprinted credentials; replace substring detection with an explicit `ENVIRONMENT` flag; purge backups from git history; move JWT secrets to the secret store.

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Dedicated-items and items-delivery static routes shadowed by `GET :id`.** `ItemsDeliveryController` registers `GET :id` (180) before `GET requests` (216), so `GET /api/items-delivery/requests` is handled as `id="requests"`. `DedicatedItemsController` registers `GET :id` (69) before `GET search` (166) **and** `GET category/:category` (147), so `GET /api/dedicated-items/search` and `GET /api/dedicated-items/category/<slug>` are unreachable (category hits `getById("category")`). Recommendation: Move all static and multi-segment routes above parameterized `:id`.

 * **Collections helper routes shadowed.** `ItemsController` `GET :collection` (43) is registered before static `GET popular-collections` (136) and `GET cache-stats` (153), so those helpers return 400 Invalid collection. `GET user-activity/:userId` is two-segment and still reachable. Recommendation: Register static paths first.

 * **Hours-report and several catch blocks lie about success.** Hours-report catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros. Posts/tasks/dedicated-items/items catch blocks leak `error.message` to clients. JwtAuthGuard `verifyFirebaseToken` also returns `error.message` (211–213). Recommendation: Fail closed; map to stable error codes; never return raw driver messages.

 * **Ride approval oversell / booking race.** Seat check uses remaining seats at book time for pending rows, but approval does not re-check capacity before commit. Two pending bookings that together exceed seats can both be approved. Recommendation: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the ride row and reject when `approved + requested > available_seats`.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang.** `authFetchInterceptor` concurrent 401s subscribe to `refreshSubscribers`. On refresh failure the leader sets `isRefreshing = false` and **clears the array without calling subscribers** (75–81), so waiters never resolve. Recommendation: Always invoke waiters (with a failure token) or reject their promises in `finally`.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** Posts-derived closed tasks use `type: 'task'`; `getTasks` fallbacks push `type: 'task_post'` (351). Dedup filters `c.type === 'task'` (324), so the two sources do not share a type and UI filters keyed on one miss the other. Recommendation: Normalize to a single type before push.

 * **Task done PATCH can duplicate completion posts.** Side-effects on mark-done are not idempotent across retries. Recommendation: Unique constraint / existence check on `(task_id, post_type=task_completion)` before insert.

 * **Sync API key is optional when left at the committed default.** `SYNC_API_KEY` defaults to `change-me-in-production`; `checkApiKey` runs only when the value is **not** that default (422–424, 511–513). Endpoints also have `AdminAuthGuard`, so this is defense-in-depth only — still fail-open if the guard is removed. Recommendation: Require a non-default key always; fail startup if unset.

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. If Admin SDK is initialized and no local row matches, `tryAutoCreateUserFromFirebase` calls `admin.auth().getUser(firebase_uid)` **without an ID token** and provisions a KC user + tokens from that UID. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body.

 * **Primary mobile login accepts email-only (password verification skipped, including empty string).** `POST /api/users/login` verifies argon2 **only when both** `loginData.password` and `user.password_hash` are truthy (223–231). Omitting `password`, sending `password: ""`, or targeting a null hash falls through to `success: true` and returns email, phone, roles, and `parent_manager_id`. `/auth/login` correctly rejects missing hash and invalid passwords. Combined with resolve-id, the documented mobile sequence is an email-only takeover. Contrasts SRS §2.1.2. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (116–118); `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (190–263, especially 223–231) -> Action: Always require a non-empty password; fail closed if hash is missing; never return `success: true` without `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated root-admin bootstrap via committed fallback email (identity-source split).** `KC_ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL_FALLBACK` is committed in `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/register` with that email sets `initialRoles = ["user", "admin"]` (101–102). Unauthenticated `POST /api/users/login` with the same email runs `array_append(roles, 'admin')` **before** the optional password check (212–220), then succeeds without a password. `getRootAdminEmail()` reads only `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` (empty if unset) and is used solely to skip `roles` writes. The PUT role/hierarchy **block** (281–297) compares a **hardcoded Gmail literal**, not the env var and not the shared constant. Setting `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` to a different mailbox does not stop bootstrap of the fallback address. - File: `apps/api/src/config/kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`; `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (101–102, 212–231); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (52–56, 281–297, 349–361) -> Action: Single source of truth for root-admin identity; require verified secret/env match plus password; never mutate roles before `argon2.verify`.

 * **Unauthenticated register binds attacker-chosen Firebase UID.** `POST /api/users/register` has no guard. INSERT writes `userData.firebase_uid || userData.id || null` into `user_profiles.firebase_uid` (139). Password is optional (97–99). The victim’s later Firebase ID-token path attaches the attacker’s KC row. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (20–33, 97–99, 139) -> Action: Ignore client `firebase_uid`/`id` on public register; require a verified Firebase ID token before binding.

 * **JWT-gated profile mass-assignment + password IDOR (`PUT /api/users/:id`).** `UpdateUserBody` accepts `password`, `roles`, and `firebase_uid`. Controller uses `JwtAuthGuard` only — **no ownership check and no `req.user` binding**. Lookup matches UUID **or** email / `firebase_uid` / `google_id` (267). Root-admin block covers roles/hierarchy only; `password` and `firebase_uid` are still written (303–347). Any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) can reset any other user’s password or steal their Firebase identity. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 267, 281–361) -> Action: Bind `:id` to `req.user.userId` unless admin; strip `password`/`roles`/`firebase_uid` from the public DTO; extend the root-admin carve-out to password and identity fields.

 * **Unauthenticated user directory and profile PII.** `GET /api/users`, `GET /api/users/:id`, `GET /api/users/search`, `GET /api/users/stats/summary`, `GET /api/users/:id/activities`, and `GET /api/users/:id/stats` have no guards. `getUserById` / `searchUsers` return email, phone, and roles. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 101–123, 135–163) -> Action: Require auth (and minimize fields) on list/search/profile reads.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** JWT-gated promote/demote/set-manager/manage-hierarchy trust body `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. `setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` **skips the admin check** when the id is omitted or not found (495–504). `manageHierarchy` (871–914) applies add/remove with no JWT binding. Unauthenticated `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, and `GET :id/hierarchy` leak the org tree. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` (492–517, 871–914, 945–980) -> Action: Bind actor to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard`; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **JWT refresh copies stale roles (SRS §2.1.4 violation).** `refreshAccessToken` copies `payload.roles` from the refresh token and never reloads `user_profiles.roles`. Demoted admins keep elevated roles until the 30-day refresh token expires or is revoked. Redis existence **is** checked in `verifyToken`. HMAC compare uses `!==` not `timingSafeEqual` (308). Missing `exp` is treated as not-expired (`payload.exp < now` is false when `exp` is undefined). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/jwt.service.ts` (146–177, 117–121, 308) -> Action: Reload roles from DB on refresh; reject tokens without `exp`; use `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **Three additional unauthenticated login paths besides resolve-id.** (1) Firebase JWKS fallback accepts any Google-signed Firebase ID token unless `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID` is set (`firebase-admin.service.ts` 11–14, 177–181); fabricated `exp=now+3600` if claim missing (216–219). (2) `POST /auth/google` falls back to `GET oauth2/v3/userinfo` with a raw Bearer access token, hardcodes `emailVerified: true`, and skips `aud` binding (613–698). (3) JwtAuthGuard Firebase fallback (117–134) then looks up `user_profiles` by `firebase_uid`. Combined with register/profile `firebase_uid` mass-assignment this is account takeover. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/firebase-admin.service.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/auth.controller.ts` (613–698); `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (117–134, 170–213) -> Action: Require `FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID`; reject access-token-only Google login; never fabricate `exp`.

 * **Shared HS256-header rate-limit bucket + unauthenticated plant-block.** `JwtAuthGuard` keys rate-limit as `token.substring(0, 16)`. Issued header is always `{alg:HS256,typ:JWT}`, so the first 16 chars are always `eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1`. 100 req/60s then 5-minute block is **global for all JWT users**, thrown as `UnauthorizedException` (401). Mobile `authFetchInterceptor` clears session on 401. Unauthenticated `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` deletes the shared window and block keys. Unauthenticated `POST /rate-limit/custom` plants `blocked:{customKey}:{identifier}` with attacker `blockDurationMs` — two calls with `{requests:1, windowMs:1000, blockDurationMs:86400000}` and `customKey=api_access` lock every JWT user for 24h. Unauthenticated `POST /rate-limit/stress-test` has unbounded `delayMs` (HTTP timeout 30s). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (96–114); `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (61–97, 155–184, 233–264) -> Action: Key by `userId` (after verify); register `APP_GUARD` ThrottlerGuard; delete or admin-guard the entire `RateLimitController`; cap `delayMs`.

 * **Primary mobile login/register unthrottled.** `ThrottlerModule` is configured (`app.module.ts` 72–77) but there is **zero** `APP_GUARD` under `apps/api/src`. Only `AuthController` and `NotificationsController` opt in. `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` (the mobile path) are unthrottled. Contrasts SRS §3.1. - File: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118) -> Action: Add global `APP_GUARD` ThrottlerGuard; decorate users login/register with a stricter `@Throttle`.

 * **Unauthenticated chat write impersonation.** Class-level `OptionalAuthGuard` always returns `true` (`jwt-auth.guard.ts` 338). Unauthenticated `POST /api/chat/messages` uses body `sender_id` (441–465). Authenticated mismatch is rejected (450–455); guests are not. Mark-read falls back to `body.user_id` (679, 759). - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 441–465, 679, 759) -> Action: Require JWT on writes; bind `sender_id` to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Spoofable social-graph and feed actors (JWT present, body wins).** Follow/unfollow use body `follower_id` not `req.user`. Post like (1345–1406), comment create (1573–1584), and comment-like (2152–2217) use body `user_id` only. Hide/update/unhide fall back to `body.user_id` when session fields are missing (2301–2305). - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts` (19–34); `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1345–1406, 1573–1584, 2152–2217, 2283–2332) -> Action: Bind actor exclusively to `req.user.userId`.

 * **Donation actor spoof, IDOR, and detail PII.** JWT-gated `POST /api/donations` uses body `donor_id` (never `req.user`) and for `type==="money"` increments that user’s `total_donations_amount` (478–534). JWT-gated `PUT`/`DELETE /api/donations/:id` have no owner/admin check (698–746). Unauthenticated `GET /api/donations/:id` returns `donor_phone` + `donor_email` (667–696). Knowledge-link POST remains public; DELETE correctly checks admin roles in-handler. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (478–534, 667–746, 233–301, 312–318) -> Action: Bind `donor_id` to JWT; owner-or-admin on mutate; redact donor contact on public GET; guard knowledge POST.

 * **Task hours spoof + hours-report IDOR.** JWT-gated `POST /api/tasks/:id/log-hours` UPSERTs as body `user_id` (384–445). JWT-gated `GET /api/tasks/hours-report/:managerId` has **no owner/admin check** (`tasks.controller.ts` 568–587; `tasks-read.service.ts` 151–311) and returns org-tree names, user IDs, hours, and task titles. Manager id resolves UUID or email/`firebase_uid`. Catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros (312–323 and controller 574–586), hiding failures. JWT-gated `GET /api/tasks` is an unscoped org-wide list (74–146). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (74–146, 384–445, 568–587); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-read.service.ts` (151–323) -> Action: Bind log-hours and report to `req.user`; scope list to assignee/admin; fail closed on report errors.

 * **NEW 2026-08-30 — Community daily-tracker IDOR.** Class is `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)`, but `GET /api/community-challenges/daily-tracker` reads `@Query("user_id")` and returns that user’s DAILY challenge rows, `current_streak` / `best_streak` / `total_entries` / `last_entry_date`, plus `entries_by_date`. Same controller already binds JWT correctly on `getUserStatistics` (`CurrentUser()`, 846–848) and `getChallengeEntries` (805–812). Concrete trigger: any JWT (including one minted by resolve-id) + `GET /api/community-challenges/daily-tracker?user_id=<victim>`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/community-group-challenges.controller.ts` (288–336 vs 805–812 / 846–848) -> Action: Ignore query `user_id`; bind to `CurrentUser().userId` (admin override only).

 * **Unauthenticated rides booking + approval oversell.** No `UseGuards` in `rides.controller.ts`. `POST :id/book` spoofs `passenger_id` (374–468). `PUT bookings/:bookingId/status` applies `status=approved` with **no capacity reject** — remaining_seats ≤ 0 only flips the ride to `full` after the fact (505–555). Concurrent books can oversell. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (374–555) -> Action: Require JWT; bind passenger to `req.user`; reject approve when remaining seats would go negative inside the same transaction.

 * **Fully unguarded write surfaces (CRM, community-members, items-delivery, dedicated-items, collections, session logout-all, places proxy).** No `UseGuards` on those controllers. Collections `POST/PUT/DELETE` write arbitrary allowlisted JSONB. Dedicated-items and items-delivery expose unauthenticated CRUD (create spoofs `owner_id`). Session `GET user/:userId`, `DELETE logout/:sessionId`, `DELETE logout-all/:userId` are unauthenticated. Places `/autocomplete`, `/place-details`, `/search-stats` proxy `GOOGLE_API_KEY`; `/place-details` interpolates `place_id` without `encodeURIComponent` (75). Redis-test (get/set/delete/keys) is registered only when `NODE_ENV !== production` (`app.module.ts` 122) — Railway `railway.toml.dev` uses `NODE_ENV=development`, so it is live on that service. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts`; `community-members.controller.ts`; `items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/{items,dedicated-items}.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–103) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard` (admin where appropriate); bind owners to JWT; encode `place_id`; disable redis-test and session demo in deployed environments.

 * **Admin-files list ACL too weak.** `GET /api/admin-files` and `GET folders` are `JwtAuthGuard` only; POST/DELETE correctly use `AdminAuthGuard`. Any authenticated user can list Finance/HR file URLs. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/admin-files.controller.ts` (80–140 vs 142–203) -> Action: Put list/folders behind `AdminAuthGuard`.

 * **Unauthenticated stats money totals + cartesian DoS.** `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` (donation money SUM) and `GET /api/stats/real-time` (`users_online` + last-hour activity) have no guards. Real-time donations/rides subquery is `FULL OUTER JOIN rides ON 1=1` (803–809) — cartesian D×R, inflated counts and an unauthenticated DoS as tables grow. `analytics/users` correctly uses `AdminAuthGuard`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (439–496, 782–831) -> Action: Guard both routes; replace `ON 1=1` with independent counts.

 * **Request-path `DROP TABLE ... CASCADE`.** `ensurePostsTable` (invoked from GET/POST post routes) can `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` when legacy columns are detected (78), plus likes/comments drops (309, 373–374). `tasks-schema.service.ts` (41) can drop `posts` via task side-effects. `DatabaseInit` drops donations/rides on startup (568, 670) when it decides the schema is legacy. Concrete trigger: any traffic that hits `ensurePostsTable` while the live table is missing `id` or `author_id`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (67–80, 176–199, 842); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Never DROP on a request path; gate destructive DDL behind an explicit offline migration.

 * **Committed credentials, backups, and env fingerprints.** `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` line 34 commits a `JWT_SECRET`. `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md` documents a production JWT secret (L102 is a placeholder). `main.ts` 149–218 fingerprints env using committed DB/Redis password/host **substrings** (do not copy values). 49 tracked JSON backups under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` include `user_profiles` keys (email/phone/`password_hash`). - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` (34); `apps/api/src/main.ts` (149–218); `apps/api/data-backups/` -> Action: Rotate all fingerprinted credentials; replace substring detection with an explicit `ENVIRONMENT` flag; purge backups from git history; move JWT secrets to the secret store.

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Dedicated-items and items-delivery static routes shadowed by `GET :id`.** `ItemsDeliveryController` registers `GET :id` (180) before `GET requests` (216), so `GET /api/items-delivery/requests` is handled as `id="requests"`. `DedicatedItemsController` registers `GET :id` (69) before `GET search` (166) **and** `GET category/:category` (147), so `GET /api/dedicated-items/search` and `GET /api/dedicated-items/category/<slug>` are unreachable (category hits `getById("category")`). Recommendation: Move all static and multi-segment routes above parameterized `:id`.

 * **Collections helper routes shadowed.** `ItemsController` `GET :collection` (43) is registered before static `GET popular-collections` (136) and `GET cache-stats` (153), so those helpers return 400 Invalid collection. `GET user-activity/:userId` is two-segment and still reachable. Recommendation: Register static paths first.

 * **Hours-report and several catch blocks lie about success.** Hours-report catch returns `success: true` with empty zeros. Posts/tasks/dedicated-items/items catch blocks leak `error.message` to clients. JwtAuthGuard `verifyFirebaseToken` also returns `error.message` (211–213). Recommendation: Fail closed; map to stable error codes; never return raw driver messages.

 * **Ride approval oversell / booking race.** Seat check uses remaining seats at book time for pending rows, but approval does not re-check capacity before commit. Two pending bookings that together exceed seats can both be approved. Recommendation: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the ride row and reject when `approved + requested > available_seats`.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang.** `authFetchInterceptor` concurrent 401s subscribe to `refreshSubscribers`. On refresh failure the leader sets `isRefreshing = false` and **clears the array without calling subscribers** (75–81), so waiters never resolve. Recommendation: Always invoke waiters (with a failure token) or reject their promises in `finally`.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** Posts-derived closed tasks use `type: 'task'`; `getTasks` fallbacks push `type: 'task_post'` (351). Dedup filters `c.type === 'task'` (324), so the two sources do not share a type and UI filters keyed on one miss the other. Recommendation: Normalize to a single type before push.

 * **Task done PATCH can duplicate completion posts.** Side-effects on mark-done are not idempotent across retries. Recommendation: Unique constraint / existence check on `(task_id, post_type=task_completion)` before insert.

 * **Sync API key is optional when left at the committed default.** `SYNC_API_KEY` defaults to `change-me-in-production`; `checkApiKey` runs only when the value is **not** that default (422–424, 511–513). Endpoints also have `AdminAuthGuard`, so this is defense-in-depth only — still fail-open if the guard is removed. Recommendation: Require a non-default key always; fail startup if unset.

**Audit branch:** `cursor/daily-repository-audit-1311`
**Scope:** Holistic security / bug / architecture / debt re-verification; secret, DDL, auth-guard, XSS/SQLi, npm, unguarded-controller, login-throttle, login-password-skip (including falsy empty-string), register identity-binding, root-admin bootstrap (including identity-source split), stats-surface, JWT-refresh-role, chat write-path, Firebase JWKS audience, JwtAuthGuard rate-limit identifier, Google OAuth access-token fallback, profile ownership/password IDOR (including root-admin carve-out), committed credential fingerprints, admin-files ACL, collections static-route shadowing, rate-limit custom-rule / stress-test, donation actor-binding / donation IDOR / donation-detail PII, task log-hours spoof, and **task hours-report IDOR / items-delivery + dedicated-items static-route shadowing**

Files >200 LOC: **196** (yesterday 195; same product tree, find methodology). Request-path XSS/SQLi patterns remain absent; rides/items-delivery `ORDER BY` columns are allowlisted.
