## 🧹 Technical Debt & Maintenance

 * **npm audit (three consecutive runs at this HEAD):** totals **45 → 95 → 96**; **critical count stable at 4**: `handlebars`, `shell-quote`, `tar`, `websocket-driver`. Registry totals remain non-deterministic for this lockfile (historical range ~45–96). Action: pin/upgrade resolving parents; report observed range, not a single count.

 * **Snyk integration removed** (commit `8e31e78`); rely on `npm audit`, Sonar under `tools/quality/sonar`, and this daily audit until a replacement OSS/SCA gate is chosen.

 * **Accidental commit artifacts still tracked:** `apps/mobile/tsc_output.txt`; `apps/mobile/android/app/debug.keystore` — remove and gitignore.

 * **`ThrottlerModule` without global `APP_GUARD`** — comments claim global 60 req/min; only Auth + Notifications apply `ThrottlerGuard`. Matches SRS §10.3 “No rate limiting on all endpoints.” Primary mobile login is therefore unthrottled (see Critical).

 * **Admin files list is JWT-only.** `GET` admin files / folders use `JwtAuthGuard` without `AdminAuthGuard` (`admin-files.controller.ts` 80–140); any authenticated user can list shared admin files. Mutations correctly require admin.

 * **JWT HMAC compare is not constant-time.** `verifyTokenSignature` uses `signature !== expectedSignature` (`jwt.service.ts:308`) instead of `crypto.timingSafeEqual`. Practical exploit over TLS is limited; still replace with a length-checked `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **JwtAuthGuard debug-logs token prefix.** `tokenPrefix: token.substring(0, 20) + "..."` (`jwt-auth.guard.ts:90–94`) can land access-token material in log aggregators. - Action: Log only token length / last 4 chars of a hash.

 * **Request-path SQL** uses parameterized `$N` in app controllers; XSS patterns (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` / runtime `eval(`) absent under `apps/` (only a comment in `parse-all-users-literal.js`). Offline scripts still interpolate table names (`import-data.ts` / `export-data.ts`). `BYPASS_AUTH` remains removed (SEC-003.1 comment in `jwt-auth.guard.ts:47`).

 * **`git diff --check`** against product tree at `3d92f9d`: clean for app sources.

 * **Prior audit PRs (#105–#133) remain unmerged** to `dev` as of 2026-08-19; product HEAD unchanged since 2026-05-04 merge #101. A dedicated hardening PR (not another report-only audit) is required to close the critical set.

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession (verified Firebase/Google ID token or existing session), not identifier matching. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body; bind any remaining resolve helper to `JwtAuthGuard`.

 * **Authenticated privilege escalation via profile mass-assignment.** `PUT /api/users/:id` is JWT-gated but does **not** check `req.user.userId === :id`. `UpdateUserBody` accepts `roles`, `firebase_uid`, `parent_manager_id`, and `hierarchy_level`. **Applied to SQL today:** `firebase_uid` (345–347) and `roles` (349–361) — any logged-in user can promote themselves (or another user) to `admin` / `super_admin` except the hardcoded root-admin email. `parent_manager_id` / `hierarchy_level` are on the DTO and used only to block that root-admin row (281–297); they are **not** written in this method (still a contract footgun). - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 281–297, 345–361); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133) -> Action: Strip privileged fields from the DTO; enforce ownership or `AdminAuthGuard`; ignore client-supplied `roles` / `firebase_uid` / hierarchy fields.

 * **Unauthenticated PII dump of all users.** `GET /api/users` and `GET /api/users/:id` (also `GET /api/users/search`) have no guards. List query selects `u.email`; get-by-id selects `email` and `phone`. Violates SRS §3.5 data minimization. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 121–123, 135–149); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (126–151, 515) -> Action: Require auth; project fields by viewer; never return email/phone to unauthorized callers.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** Hierarchy mutate routes use `JwtAuthGuard` but authorization is based on **body** `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. Omitting `requestingUserId` on `setManager` skips the admin check (`setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` returns `null` when the id is omitted **or** when the supplied id is not found). An attacker with any valid JWT can send another admin’s UUID and promote/demote. Unauthenticated reads: `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, `GET :id/hierarchy`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` (492–517, 871–914, 945–980) -> Action: Bind actor exclusively to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard` (or volunteer-manager policy) on mutations; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **Spoofable follow graph.** `POST/DELETE /api/users/:id/follow` is JWT-gated but inserts `followData.follower_id` from the body, not `req.user.userId`. Any authenticated user can create or delete follows as any other user. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts` (6–8, 19–34); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (165–180) -> Action: Use `req.user.userId` as follower; ignore body actor ids.

 * **Chat is effectively public (reads and writes).** Class-level `@UseGuards(OptionalAuthGuard)` **always returns `true`** (`jwt-auth.guard.ts:338`). Guest path for conversations uses the URL `userId` (`chat.controller.ts:267–268`). Guest path for messages skips participant checks (`378–387`) and returns conversation messages. **Write path (re-verified 2026-08-18):** unauthenticated `POST` chat `/messages` requires `sender_id` and then sends as that user (`441–465`) — concrete trigger: `POST /messages` with a victim UUID impersonates them. Mark-read endpoints fall back to `body.user_id` (`679`, `759`). - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (308–339); `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 250–270, 378–387, 441–465, 671–679, 751–759) -> Action: Require `JwtAuthGuard`; always bind sender/reader to `req.user`; never accept body/URL actor ids.

 * **Rides: no auth; spoofable passenger; unauthenticated booking status.** `rides.controller.ts` has **zero** `@UseGuards`. `POST :id/book` uses body `passenger_id` (email / firebase_uid / UUID). `PUT bookings/:bookingId/status` updates status from the body with no driver/admin check. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (37, 374–468, 505–555) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard`; bind passenger to `req.user`; restrict status changes to driver/admin; reject approve when remaining seats would go negative (see Bugs).

 * **Unauthenticated mutating admin/CRM/community/items surfaces.** No `@UseGuards` on: `CrmController` (`/api/crm`), `CommunityMembersController` (`/api/community-members`, includes `contact_info` email/phone), `ItemsDeliveryController` (`/api/items-delivery`, client `owner_id`), `DedicatedItemsController` (`/api/dedicated-items` create/update/delete), `ItemsController` (`/api/collections/:collection` CRUD allowlist includes `users`, `chats`, `messages`, `tasks`, `notifications`, `rides`, `organizations`). Concrete trigger: unauthenticated `POST /api/crm`, `DELETE /api/community-members/:id`, `POST /api/collections/messages/...`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts`; `community-members.controller.ts`; `items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/dedicated-items.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/items.controller.ts` + `items.service.ts` (15–36) -> Action: Auth + role/ownership on all mutating routes; lock down or remove generic collections CRUD.

 * **Unauthenticated Redis session/token store access (non-production).** `RedisTestController` (`/redis-test`) has no guards. `app.module.ts:122` registers it when `NODE_ENV !== "production"`. `railway.toml.dev` sets `NODE_ENV = "development"`, so the **dev Railway service** exposes `GET /redis-test/keys?pattern=*`, `GET /redis-test/get/:key`, `POST /redis-test/set`, `DELETE /redis-test/delete/:key` against the session/token store. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/redis-test.controller.ts` (16–124); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (122) -> Action: Remove from all deployed builds (including development) or gate with `AdminAuthGuard` + local-only bind.

 * **Unauthenticated rate-limit bypass.** `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` (and test/stats/rules/custom/simulate) has no auth. An attacker can clear their own (or any) identifier’s throttle. Combined with missing global `APP_GUARD`, SRS §3.1 “global 60 req/60s via ThrottlerGuard” is not actually enforced. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (20–175); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77) — `ThrottlerModule.forRoot` present, **no `APP_GUARD` / `ThrottlerGuard` registration anywhere in `apps/api/src`** -> Action: Register global `ThrottlerGuard`; delete or admin-gate the debug controller.

 * **Unauthenticated session enumeration and forced logout.** Demo `SessionController` (`/session`) has no guards. `POST /session/login` verifies real `password_hash` via argon2. `GET /session/user/:userId` lists sessions; `DELETE /session/logout/:sessionId` and `DELETE /session/logout-all/:userId` destroy sessions without proving possession. Concrete trigger: `DELETE /session/logout-all/<victim-uuid>` signs the victim out of all devices. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts` (27–229) -> Action: Remove from production/dev deployments; if kept for tests, bind to `JwtAuthGuard` + ownership and disable login.

 * **Unauthenticated Google Places proxy (quota / cost).** `PlacesController` has no guards. `GET /autocomplete`, `GET /place-details`, and `GET /search-stats` call Google with `process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY` (or dump Redis search-term analytics). Concrete trigger: unauthenticated high-volume `GET /autocomplete` or `/place-details` burns billed quota and can probe the key’s validity via error bodies. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–87, 88–103) -> Action: Require auth (or a tight per-IP throttle + app-check); never expose the raw Google error/key status; consider Maps SDK on-device instead of a server proxy for public traffic.

 * **Request-path and startup `DROP TABLE … CASCADE`.** `GET /api/posts` calls `ensurePostsTable()` → `migrateExistingPostsTableColumns`; if `id` or `author_id` is missing, it runs `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` on a user request. `ensureLikesCommentsTable` (also on that GET) drops `post_likes` when `id` is missing (309) and `comment_likes` + `post_comments` (373–374). Task side-effects call `TasksSchemaService.ensurePostsTable()` which drops `posts` when `author_id` is absent. `DatabaseInit.onModuleInit` drops `donations` / `rides` if a legacy JSONB `data` column exists. Concrete trigger: schema drift or a malicious/failed migration that removes a column, then any public GET posts or API boot wipes related tables. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (67–78, 176–199, 305–374, 830–842); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (18–41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Move DDL to versioned migrations; never DROP on a request path; refuse boot on unexpected schema instead of destroying data.

 * **Committed credentials and backups.** Tracked `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` contains a live-format `JWT_SECRET`. Runbook `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md:126` embeds a production-labeled `JWT_SECRET` (value not repeated here). 49 JSON files under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` are tracked; `user_profiles.json` schema includes `email`, `phone`, `password_hash`. - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev:34`; `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md:126`; `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/*` -> Action: Rotate all exposed JWT secrets; purge secrets from git history; gitignore backups; use secret managers only.

 * **JWT-gated but unscoped task list.** `GET /api/tasks` uses `JwtAuthGuard` then lists by query filters (`assignee`, `status`, …) with **no** restriction to the caller’s tasks. Any authenticated user can enumerate all tasks (ClosedRoute even queries another profile’s `assignee`). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (74–146) -> Action: Default-scope to `req.user`; require admin for global lists.

 * **Unthrottled primary mobile login/register.** SRS §2.1.2 and §3.1 require `ThrottlerGuard` on auth. `AuthController` (`/auth/*`) has class-level `ThrottlerGuard`, but the **primary mobile path** (`apiService.ts` / `dbConfig.USER_LOGIN`) calls `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` on `UsersController`, which have **no** throttle and no global `APP_GUARD`. Concrete trigger: unbounded credential stuffing against `/api/users/login` (and account-creation spam on `/api/users/register`) bypasses the documented 60 req/60s auth limit. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118); `apps/mobile/utils/apiService.ts` (~464); `apps/mobile/utils/dbConfig.ts` (44); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77) -> Action: Apply `ThrottlerGuard` (stricter login tier) to `/api/users/login` and `/register`; register global `APP_GUARD`; keep `/auth/*` and `/api/users/*` auth surfaces consistent.

 * **JWT refresh persists stale roles.** SRS §2.1.4 requires refresh to **fetch latest roles from the database** so demotions propagate. `JwtService.refreshAccessToken` copies `payload.roles` from the presented refresh token and issues a new access token with those roles; there is no `user_profiles` lookup. Redis existence of the refresh token **is** checked inside `verifyToken` (123–129). Concrete trigger: an admin is demoted to `user` while still holding a valid 30-day refresh token; `POST /auth/refresh` continues to mint access tokens with `roles: ["admin"]` until that refresh token expires or is revoked in Redis. Complements profile mass-assignment (attacker can also *gain* roles) by letting a formerly privileged user *keep* them after a legitimate demotion. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/jwt.service.ts` (146–177, especially 157–165); `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/auth.controller.ts` (981–998) -> Action: On refresh, load `roles` from `user_profiles` by `payload.userId`; reject refresh if the user is inactive; consider rotating the refresh token.

---

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Ride booking approval can oversell seats.** `updateBookingStatus` sets status to `approved` then only marks the ride `full` when remaining ≤ 0; no capacity reject and no `FOR UPDATE` row lock before approve. Concurrent approvals can exceed `available_seats`. Booking create does check remaining seats (407–410) but that check is not applied on approve. - Recommendation: In one transaction, lock the ride + sum approved seats; reject approve when `seats_requested` would exceed remaining capacity. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (505–555).

 * **Task done PATCH can create duplicate completion posts.** `evaluateMarkDoneTimeLogRequirement` skips when `oldStatus === "done"`, but `runUpdateTaskCompletionPosts` still runs whenever `body.status === "done"` (`tasks-patch-mutation.service.ts:380`) with no transition check. `finalizePerformerCompletionPost` INSERTs a new `task_completion` row when no matching `task_assignment` title is updated, so a second PATCH to `done` duplicates the feed post. - Recommendation: Gate completion side effects on `oldStatus !== "done"`; add an idempotent unique constraint / upsert for completion posts.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang on failure.** `apps/mobile/auth/interceptors/authFetchInterceptor.ts` (~75–89): failed refresh clears `refreshSubscribers` without resolving waiters subscribed via `subscribeTokenRefresh`. Parallel 401s wait forever. - Recommendation: Reject/resolve all subscribers on failure paths before clearing tokens.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** `ClosedRoute.tsx` filters existing content with `type === 'task'` (~324) but pushes `type: 'task_post'` (~351), so duplicate suppression against post-derived tasks is ineffective. - Recommendation: Align filter and push types (or normalize via a single type constant matching `FeedItem.type`).

 * **Spoofable post comment author.** `POST /api/posts/:postId/comments` is JWT-gated but uses body `user_id`, not `req.user.userId`. Any authenticated user can attribute comments to another account. - Recommendation: Bind `user_id` exclusively from the JWT. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1573–1584).

 * **Unauthenticated knowledge-link writes.** `POST /api/donations/knowledge/links` is explicitly public and inserts caller-supplied URL/display name (optional spoofed `createdByUserId`). Spam/defacement surface. DELETE is JWT-gated and **does** check `admin` / `super_admin` / `org_admin` in-handler (312–318) — decorator is JWT-only. - Recommendation: Require auth; rate-limit; ignore client `createdByUserId`. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (226–291, 304–318).

 * **`PUT /api/posts/:postId` owner check falls back to body `user_id`.** Guard sets `req.user.userId` (JWT payload), so the fallback is currently dead in the happy path; if the payload shape ever omitted `userId`/`id`/`sub`, a caller could pass another user’s id and pass the `author_id` check. - Recommendation: Remove `body.user_id` fallback; compare `req.user.userId` only. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (2283–2332). (The 2026-05-04 `images` TEXT[] mapping in commit `073722f` is otherwise correct.)

 * **Unauthenticated stats analytics / real-time.** `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` has no guard and returns `SUM` of money donations plus weekly/monthly counts. `GET /api/stats/real-time` has no guard and returns last-hour `user_activities` counts plus `users_online` (profiles with `last_active` in 5 minutes). `GET /api/stats/analytics/users` is correctly `AdminAuthGuard`. Concrete trigger: unauthenticated scrape of donation-money totals and live activity. - Recommendation: Auth or admin-gate money/activity analytics; keep only coarse public community counters unauthenticated. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (439–496, 782–831).

 * **`SYNC_API_KEY` defaults to `change-me-in-production`.** Sync mutate routes also use `AdminAuthGuard`, so this is not a standalone bypass today, but a missing env var leaves a known default and the extra key check is skipped when the default is still in place. - Recommendation: Fail closed if unset in production. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/sync.controller.ts` (27–40, 422–424, 511–513).

 * **Client-facing SQL/internal error strings on posts (new 2026-08-18).** Several `posts.controller` catch blocks return `` `Failed to …: ${errorMessage}` `` to the HTTP client (e.g. get-user-posts 1168–1182, add-comment 1704–1716). Stack traces stay in logs (good), but `error.message` can include Postgres detail. Violates `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/constraints.md` (no technical leaks to clients). - Recommendation: Return a generic client error; log `error.message` server-side only.

---

## 🏗️ Architecture & Clean Code

 * **Clean Architecture layers are absent.** No `/domain`, `/application`, or `/infrastructure` folders under `apps/` or `packages/` (count 0). Target in `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/layers-and-boundaries.md` (Infrastructure → Application → Domain inward) is not met. Domain logic lives in Nest controllers/services that import `pg` `Pool`, Redis, and HTTP decorators.

 * **Closest pure islands** (no Nest/`pg` imports): `apps/api/src/tasks/task-completion-participants.ts`, `tasks-validation.ts`, `tasks-query-params.ts`, `tasks.types.ts`. `tasks.controller.ts` still injects `Pool`.

 * **Fat HTTP/SQL controllers** (far above the ~200 LOC guideline in `constraints.md`): `posts.controller.ts` 2741, `stats.controller.ts` 1709, `user-hierarchy.service.ts` 1675, `database.init.ts` 1374, `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` 1097, `donations.controller.ts` 892, `chat.controller.ts` 813, `rides.controller.ts` 779. `items-delivery.service.ts` remains misplaced under `controllers/`.

 * **Mobile debt hubs:** `utils/databaseService.ts` (1226), `apiService.ts` (1055), `enhancedDatabaseService.ts` (897); duplicate `google_auth/SecureApiService.ts` (1165). Duplicate feed mappers (`mapPostToFeedItemForItemsScreen` / `ForTrumpScreen` vs `useFeedData`) remain logged as follow-ups in `tech-debt-log.md`. **196** TypeScript source files exceed ~200 LOC (apps+packages, excluding `node_modules`/`dist`/spec files).

 * **SRS vs code (unchanged):** Operator matching module, anonymity_level column, and `/api/operator/*` still missing (`docs/SSOT/SRS/10-gaps-and-assumptions.md`). OptionalAuthGuard’s always-true behavior contradicts SRS §3.2 (“enriches authenticated requests” while allowing guests — it does not protect private chat). Dual login surfaces (`/auth/login` throttled vs `/api/users/login` unthrottled) contradict SRS §2.1.2. JWT refresh without DB role reload contradicts SRS §2.1.4.

 * **Hardcoded root-admin email in request-path SQL.** `user-profile.service.ts` special-cases `karmacommunity2.0@gmail.com` in SELECT/UPDATE (lines 134, 181, 281–289, 519–524) instead of `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` / `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Contradicts SRS §3.2 SEC-003.1 (“Admin access is role-based only — no hardcoded email checks”).

## 🧹 Technical Debt & Maintenance

 * **npm audit (three consecutive runs at this HEAD):** totals **45 → 95 → 96**; **critical count stable at 4**: `handlebars`, `shell-quote`, `tar`, `websocket-driver`. Registry totals remain non-deterministic for this lockfile (historical range ~45–96). Action: pin/upgrade resolving parents; report observed range, not a single count.

 * **Snyk integration removed** (commit `8e31e78`); rely on `npm audit`, Sonar under `tools/quality/sonar`, and this daily audit until a replacement OSS/SCA gate is chosen.

 * **Accidental commit artifacts still tracked:** `apps/mobile/tsc_output.txt`; `apps/mobile/android/app/debug.keystore` — remove and gitignore.

 * **`ThrottlerModule` without global `APP_GUARD`** — comments claim global 60 req/min; only Auth + Notifications apply `ThrottlerGuard`. Matches SRS §10.3 “No rate limiting on all endpoints.” Primary mobile login is therefore unthrottled (see Critical).

 * **Admin files list is JWT-only.** `GET` admin files / folders use `JwtAuthGuard` without `AdminAuthGuard` (`admin-files.controller.ts` 80–140); any authenticated user can list shared admin files. Mutations correctly require admin.

 * **JWT HMAC compare is not constant-time.** `verifyTokenSignature` uses `signature !== expectedSignature` (`jwt.service.ts:308`) instead of `crypto.timingSafeEqual`. Practical exploit over TLS is limited; still replace with a length-checked `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **Request-path SQL** uses parameterized `$N` in app controllers; XSS patterns (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` / runtime `eval(`) absent under `apps/` (only a comment in `parse-all-users-literal.js`). Offline scripts still interpolate table names (`import-data.ts` / `export-data.ts`). `BYPASS_AUTH` remains removed (SEC-003.1 comment in `jwt-auth.guard.ts:47`).

 * **`git diff --check`** against product tree at `3d92f9d`: clean for app sources.

 * **Prior audit PRs (#105–#133) remain unmerged** to `dev` as of 2026-08-18; product HEAD unchanged since 2026-05-04 merge #101. A dedicated hardening PR (not another report-only audit) is required to close the critical set.

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession (verified Firebase/Google ID token or existing session), not identifier matching. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body; bind any remaining resolve helper to `JwtAuthGuard`.

 * **Authenticated privilege escalation via profile mass-assignment.** `PUT /api/users/:id` is JWT-gated but does **not** check `req.user.userId === :id`. `UpdateUserBody` accepts `roles`, `firebase_uid`, `parent_manager_id`, and `hierarchy_level`. **Applied to SQL today:** `firebase_uid` (345–347) and `roles` (349–361) — any logged-in user can promote themselves (or another user) to `admin` / `super_admin` except the hardcoded root-admin email. `parent_manager_id` / `hierarchy_level` are on the DTO and used only to block that root-admin row (281–297); they are **not** written in this method (still a contract footgun). - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 281–297, 345–361); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133) -> Action: Strip privileged fields from the DTO; enforce ownership or `AdminAuthGuard`; ignore client-supplied `roles` / `firebase_uid` / hierarchy fields.

 * **Unauthenticated PII dump of all users.** `GET /api/users` and `GET /api/users/:id` (also `GET /api/users/search`) have no guards. List query selects `u.email`; get-by-id selects `email` and `phone`. Violates SRS §3.5 data minimization. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 121–123, 135–149); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (126–151, 515) -> Action: Require auth; project fields by viewer; never return email/phone to unauthorized callers.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** Hierarchy mutate routes use `JwtAuthGuard` but authorization is based on **body** `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. Omitting `requestingUserId` on `setManager` skips the admin check (`setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` returns `null` when the id is omitted). An attacker with any valid JWT can send another admin’s UUID and promote/demote. Unauthenticated reads: `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, `GET :id/hierarchy`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` (492–517, 871–914, 945–980) -> Action: Bind actor exclusively to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard` (or volunteer-manager policy) on mutations; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **Spoofable follow graph.** `POST/DELETE /api/users/:id/follow` is JWT-gated but inserts `followData.follower_id` from the body, not `req.user.userId`. Any authenticated user can create or delete follows as any other user. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts` (6–8, 19–34); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (165–180) -> Action: Use `req.user.userId` as follower; ignore body actor ids.

 * **Chat is effectively public.** Class-level `@UseGuards(OptionalAuthGuard)` **always returns `true`** (`jwt-auth.guard.ts:338`). Guest path for conversations uses the URL `userId` (`chat.controller.ts:267–268`). Guest path for messages skips participant checks (`378–387`) and returns conversation messages. Concrete trigger: unauthenticated `GET` of another user’s conversations or any conversation’s messages. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (308–339); `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 250–270, 378–387) -> Action: Require `JwtAuthGuard`; always enforce participant membership from `req.user`.

 * **Rides: no auth; spoofable passenger; unauthenticated booking status.** `rides.controller.ts` has **zero** `@UseGuards`. `POST :id/book` uses body `passenger_id` (email / firebase_uid / UUID). `PUT bookings/:bookingId/status` updates status from the body with no driver/admin check. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (37, 374–468, 505–555) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard`; bind passenger to `req.user`; restrict status changes to driver/admin; reject approve when remaining seats would go negative (see Bugs).

 * **Unauthenticated mutating admin/CRM/community/items surfaces.** No `@UseGuards` on: `CrmController` (`/api/crm`), `CommunityMembersController` (`/api/community-members`, includes `contact_info` email/phone), `ItemsDeliveryController` (`/api/items-delivery`, client `owner_id`), `DedicatedItemsController` (`/api/dedicated-items` create/update/delete), `ItemsController` (`/api/collections/:collection` CRUD allowlist includes `users`, `chats`, `messages`, `tasks`, `notifications`, `rides`, `organizations`). Concrete trigger: unauthenticated `POST /api/crm`, `DELETE /api/community-members/:id`, `POST /api/collections/messages/...`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts`; `community-members.controller.ts`; `items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/dedicated-items.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/items.controller.ts` + `items.service.ts` (15–36) -> Action: Auth + role/ownership on all mutating routes; lock down or remove generic collections CRUD.

 * **Unauthenticated Redis session/token store access (non-production).** `RedisTestController` (`/redis-test`) has no guards. `app.module.ts:122` registers it when `NODE_ENV !== "production"`. `railway.toml.dev` sets `NODE_ENV = "development"`, so the **dev Railway service** exposes `GET /redis-test/keys?pattern=*`, `GET /redis-test/get/:key`, `POST /redis-test/set`, `DELETE /redis-test/delete/:key` against the session/token store. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/redis-test.controller.ts` (16–124); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (122) -> Action: Remove from all deployed builds (including development) or gate with `AdminAuthGuard` + local-only bind.

 * **Unauthenticated rate-limit bypass.** `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` (and test/stats/rules/custom/simulate) has no auth. An attacker can clear their own (or any) identifier’s throttle. Combined with missing global `APP_GUARD`, SRS §3.1 “global 60 req/60s via ThrottlerGuard” is not actually enforced. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (20–175); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77) — `ThrottlerModule.forRoot` present, **no `APP_GUARD` / `ThrottlerGuard` registration anywhere in `apps/api/src`** -> Action: Register global `ThrottlerGuard`; delete or admin-gate the debug controller.

 * **Unauthenticated session enumeration and forced logout.** Demo `SessionController` (`/session`) has no guards. `POST /session/login` verifies real `password_hash` via argon2. `GET /session/user/:userId` lists sessions; `DELETE /session/logout/:sessionId` and `DELETE /session/logout-all/:userId` destroy sessions without proving possession. Concrete trigger: `DELETE /session/logout-all/<victim-uuid>` signs the victim out of all devices. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts` (27–229) -> Action: Remove from production/dev deployments; if kept for tests, bind to `JwtAuthGuard` + ownership and disable login.

 * **Unauthenticated Google Places proxy (quota / cost).** `PlacesController` has no guards. `GET /autocomplete`, `GET /place-details`, and `GET /search-stats` call Google with `process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY` (or dump Redis search-term analytics). Concrete trigger: unauthenticated high-volume `GET /autocomplete` or `/place-details` burns billed quota and can probe the key’s validity via error bodies. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–87, 88–103) -> Action: Require auth (or a tight per-IP throttle + app-check); never expose the raw Google error/key status; consider Maps SDK on-device instead of a server proxy for public traffic.

 * **Request-path and startup `DROP TABLE … CASCADE`.** `GET /api/posts` calls `ensurePostsTable()` → `migrateExistingPostsTableColumns`; if `id` or `author_id` is missing, it runs `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` on a user request. Task side-effects call `TasksSchemaService.ensurePostsTable()` which drops `posts` when `author_id` is absent. `DatabaseInit.onModuleInit` drops `donations` / `rides` if a legacy JSONB `data` column exists. Concrete trigger: schema drift or a malicious/failed migration that removes a column, then any public GET posts or API boot wipes related tables. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (67–78, 176–199, 830–842); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (18–41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Move DDL to versioned migrations; never DROP on a request path; refuse boot on unexpected schema instead of destroying data.

 * **Committed credentials and backups.** Tracked `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` contains a live-format `JWT_SECRET`. Runbook `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md:126` embeds a production-labeled `JWT_SECRET` (value not repeated here). 49 JSON files under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` are tracked; `user_profiles.json` schema includes `email`, `phone`, `password_hash`. - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev:34`; `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md:126`; `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/*` -> Action: Rotate all exposed JWT secrets; purge secrets from git history; gitignore backups; use secret managers only.

 * **JWT-gated but unscoped task list.** `GET /api/tasks` uses `JwtAuthGuard` then lists by query filters (`assignee`, `status`, …) with **no** restriction to the caller’s tasks. Any authenticated user can enumerate all tasks (ClosedRoute even queries another profile’s `assignee`). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (74–146) -> Action: Default-scope to `req.user`; require admin for global lists.

 * **Unthrottled primary mobile login/register.** SRS §2.1.2 and §3.1 require `ThrottlerGuard` on auth. `AuthController` (`/auth/*`) has class-level `ThrottlerGuard`, but the **primary mobile path** (`apiService.ts` / `dbConfig.USER_LOGIN`) calls `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` on `UsersController`, which have **no** throttle and no global `APP_GUARD`. Concrete trigger: unbounded credential stuffing against `/api/users/login` (and account-creation spam on `/api/users/register`) bypasses the documented 60 req/60s auth limit. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118); `apps/mobile/utils/apiService.ts` (~464); `apps/mobile/utils/dbConfig.ts` (44); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77) -> Action: Apply `ThrottlerGuard` (stricter login tier) to `/api/users/login` and `/register`; register global `APP_GUARD`; keep `/auth/*` and `/api/users/*` auth surfaces consistent.

 * **JWT refresh persists stale roles (new 2026-08-17).** SRS §2.1.4 requires refresh to **fetch latest roles from the database** so demotions propagate. `JwtService.refreshAccessToken` copies `payload.roles` from the presented refresh token and issues a new access token with those roles; there is no `user_profiles` lookup. Concrete trigger: an admin is demoted to `user` while still holding a valid 30-day refresh token; `POST /auth/refresh` continues to mint access tokens with `roles: ["admin"]` until that refresh token expires or is revoked in Redis. Complements profile mass-assignment (attacker can also *gain* roles) by letting a formerly privileged user *keep* them after a legitimate demotion. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/services/jwt.service.ts` (146–177, especially 157–165); `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/auth.controller.ts` (981–998) -> Action: On refresh, load `roles` from `user_profiles` by `payload.userId`; reject refresh if the user is inactive; consider rotating the refresh token.

---

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Ride booking approval can oversell seats.** `updateBookingStatus` sets status to `approved` then only marks the ride `full` when remaining ≤ 0; no capacity reject and no `FOR UPDATE` row lock before approve. Concurrent approvals can exceed `available_seats`. - Recommendation: In one transaction, lock the ride + sum approved seats; reject approve when `seats_requested` would exceed remaining capacity. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (505–555).

 * **Task done PATCH can create duplicate completion posts.** `evaluateMarkDoneTimeLogRequirement` skips when `oldStatus === "done"`, but `runUpdateTaskCompletionPosts` still runs whenever `body.status === "done"` (`tasks-patch-mutation.service.ts:380`) with no transition check. `finalizePerformerCompletionPost` INSERTs a new `task_completion` row when no matching `task_assignment` title is updated, so a second PATCH to `done` duplicates the feed post. - Recommendation: Gate completion side effects on `oldStatus !== "done"`; add an idempotent unique constraint / upsert for completion posts.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang on failure.** `apps/mobile/auth/interceptors/authFetchInterceptor.ts` (~75–89): failed refresh clears `refreshSubscribers` without resolving waiters subscribed via `subscribeTokenRefresh`. Parallel 401s wait forever. - Recommendation: Reject/resolve all subscribers on failure paths before clearing tokens.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** `ClosedRoute.tsx` filters existing content with `type === 'task'` (~324) but pushes `type: 'task_post'` (~351), so duplicate suppression against post-derived tasks is ineffective. - Recommendation: Align filter and push types (or normalize via a single type constant matching `FeedItem.type`).

 * **Spoofable post comment author.** `POST /api/posts/:postId/comments` is JWT-gated but uses body `user_id`, not `req.user.userId`. Any authenticated user can attribute comments to another account. - Recommendation: Bind `user_id` exclusively from the JWT. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1573–1584).

 * **Unauthenticated knowledge-link writes.** `POST /api/donations/knowledge/links` is explicitly public and inserts caller-supplied URL/display name (optional spoofed `createdByUserId`). Spam/defacement surface. DELETE is JWT-gated and **does** check `admin` / `super_admin` / `org_admin` in-handler (312–318) — decorator is JWT-only. - Recommendation: Require auth; rate-limit; ignore client `createdByUserId`. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (226–291, 304–318).

 * **`PUT /api/posts/:postId` owner check falls back to body `user_id`.** Guard sets `req.user.userId` (JWT payload), so the fallback is currently dead in the happy path; if the payload shape ever omitted `userId`/`id`/`sub`, a caller could pass another user’s id and pass the `author_id` check. - Recommendation: Remove `body.user_id` fallback; compare `req.user.userId` only. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (2283–2332). (The 2026-05-04 `images` TEXT[] mapping in commit `073722f` is otherwise correct.)

 * **Unauthenticated stats analytics / real-time.** `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` has no guard and returns `SUM` of money donations plus weekly/monthly counts. `GET /api/stats/real-time` has no guard and returns last-hour `user_activities` counts plus `users_online` (profiles with `last_active` in 5 minutes). `GET /api/stats/analytics/users` is correctly `AdminAuthGuard`. Concrete trigger: unauthenticated scrape of donation-money totals and live activity. - Recommendation: Auth or admin-gate money/activity analytics; keep only coarse public community counters unauthenticated. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (439–496, 782–831).

 * **`SYNC_API_KEY` defaults to `change-me-in-production`.** Sync mutate routes also use `AdminAuthGuard`, so this is not a standalone bypass today, but a missing env var leaves a known default and the extra key check is skipped when the default is still in place. - Recommendation: Fail closed if unset in production. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/sync.controller.ts` (27–40, 422–424, 511–513).

---

## 🏗️ Architecture & Clean Code

 * **Clean Architecture layers are absent.** No `/domain`, `/application`, or `/infrastructure` folders under `apps/` or `packages/` (count 0). Target in `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/layers-and-boundaries.md` (Infrastructure → Application → Domain inward) is not met. Domain logic lives in Nest controllers/services that import `pg` `Pool`, Redis, and HTTP decorators.

 * **Closest pure islands** (no Nest/`pg` imports): `apps/api/src/tasks/task-completion-participants.ts`, `tasks-validation.ts`, `tasks-query-params.ts`, `tasks.types.ts`. `tasks.controller.ts` still injects `Pool`.

 * **Fat HTTP/SQL controllers** (far above the ~200 LOC guideline in `constraints.md`): `posts.controller.ts` 2741, `stats.controller.ts` 1709, `user-hierarchy.service.ts` 1675, `database.init.ts` 1374, `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` 1097, `donations.controller.ts` 892, `chat.controller.ts` 813, `rides.controller.ts` 779. `items-delivery.service.ts` remains misplaced under `controllers/`.

 * **Mobile debt hubs:** `utils/databaseService.ts` (1225), `apiService.ts` (1055), `enhancedDatabaseService.ts` (897); duplicate `google_auth/SecureApiService.ts` (1165). Duplicate feed mappers (`mapPostToFeedItemForItemsScreen` / `ForTrumpScreen` vs `useFeedData`) remain logged as follow-ups in `tech-debt-log.md`. **196** TypeScript source files exceed ~200 LOC (apps+packages, excluding `node_modules`/`dist`/spec files).

 * **SRS vs code (unchanged):** Operator matching module, anonymity_level column, and `/api/operator/*` still missing (`docs/SSOT/SRS/10-gaps-and-assumptions.md`). OptionalAuthGuard’s always-true behavior contradicts SRS §3.2 (“enriches authenticated requests” while allowing guests — it does not protect private chat). Dual login surfaces (`/auth/login` throttled vs `/api/users/login` unthrottled) contradict SRS §2.1.2. JWT refresh without DB role reload contradicts SRS §2.1.4.

 * **Hardcoded root-admin email in request-path SQL.** `user-profile.service.ts` special-cases `karmacommunity2.0@gmail.com` in SELECT/UPDATE (lines 134, 181, 281–289, 519–524) instead of `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` / `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Contradicts SRS §3.2 SEC-003.1 (“Admin access is role-based only — no hardcoded email checks”).

## 🧹 Technical Debt & Maintenance

 * **npm audit (three consecutive runs at this HEAD):** totals **45 → 95 → 96**; **critical count stable at 4**: `handlebars`, `shell-quote`, `tar`, `websocket-driver`. Registry totals remain non-deterministic for this lockfile (historical range ~45–96). Action: pin/upgrade resolving parents; report observed range, not a single count.

 * **Snyk integration removed** (commit `8e31e78`); rely on `npm audit`, Sonar under `tools/quality/sonar`, and this daily audit until a replacement OSS/SCA gate is chosen.

 * **Accidental commit artifacts still tracked:** `apps/mobile/tsc_output.txt`; `apps/mobile/android/app/debug.keystore` — remove and gitignore.

 * **`ThrottlerModule` without global `APP_GUARD`** — comments claim global 60 req/min; only Auth + Notifications apply `ThrottlerGuard`. Matches SRS §10.3 “No rate limiting on all endpoints.” Primary mobile login is therefore unthrottled (see Critical).

 * **Admin files list is JWT-only.** `GET` admin files / folders use `JwtAuthGuard` without `AdminAuthGuard` (`admin-files.controller.ts` 80–140); any authenticated user can list shared admin files. Mutations correctly require admin.

 * **JWT HMAC compare is not constant-time.** `verifyTokenSignature` uses `signature !== expectedSignature` (`jwt.service.ts:308`) instead of `crypto.timingSafeEqual`. Practical exploit over TLS is limited; still replace with a length-checked `timingSafeEqual`.

 * **Request-path SQL** uses parameterized `$N` in app controllers; XSS patterns (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` / runtime `eval(`) absent under `apps/` (only a comment in `parse-all-users-literal.js`). Offline scripts still interpolate table names (`import-data.ts` / `export-data.ts`). `BYPASS_AUTH` remains removed (SEC-003.1 comment in `jwt-auth.guard.ts:47`).

 * **`git diff --check`** against product tree at `3d92f9d`: clean for app sources.

 * **Prior audit PRs (#105–#132) remain unmerged** to `dev` as of 2026-08-17; product HEAD unchanged since 2026-05-04 merge #101. A dedicated hardening PR (not another report-only audit) is required to close the critical set.

## 🚨 Critical Vulnerabilities

 * **Unauthenticated identifier-only account takeover via `POST /api/users/resolve-id`.** No `JwtAuthGuard`. Supplying a known `email`, `google_id`, or `firebase_uid` looks up `user_profiles`, optionally **links** a caller-supplied `firebase_uid`/`google_id` onto that row, then **mints a full JWT pair** (`accessToken` + `refreshToken`) for that user. Concrete trigger: `POST /api/users/resolve-id` with `{ "email": "<victim>" }` returns tokens with the victim’s `roles`. SRS §2.1.6 currently documents this minting behavior — the spec itself should be tightened to require proof of possession (verified Firebase/Google ID token or existing session), not identifier matching. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (183–188) → `apps/api/src/services/user-auth.service.ts` (`resolveUserId` 270–342, `linkResolveIdentifiers` 580–627, `buildResolveSuccessResponse` 645–672) -> Action: Remove token minting from resolve-id; require a verified ID token; never link identifiers from an unauthenticated body; bind any remaining resolve helper to `JwtAuthGuard`.

 * **Authenticated privilege escalation via profile mass-assignment.** `PUT /api/users/:id` is JWT-gated but does **not** check `req.user.userId === :id`. `UpdateUserBody` accepts `roles`, `firebase_uid`, `parent_manager_id`, and `hierarchy_level`. **Applied to SQL today:** `firebase_uid` (345–347) and `roles` (349–361) — any logged-in user can promote themselves (or another user) to `admin` / `super_admin` except the hardcoded root-admin email. `parent_manager_id` / `hierarchy_level` are on the DTO and used only to block that root-admin row (281–297); they are **not** written in this method (still a contract footgun). - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (18–32, 281–297, 345–361); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (126–133) -> Action: Strip privileged fields from the DTO; enforce ownership or `AdminAuthGuard`; ignore client-supplied `roles` / `firebase_uid` / hierarchy fields.

 * **Unauthenticated PII dump of all users.** `GET /api/users` and `GET /api/users/:id` (also `GET /api/users/search`) have no guards. List query selects `u.email`; get-by-id selects `email` and `phone`. Violates SRS §3.5 data minimization. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (40–43, 121–123, 135–149); `apps/api/src/services/user-profile.service.ts` (126–151, 515) -> Action: Require auth; project fields by viewer; never return email/phone to unauthorized callers.

 * **Client-supplied hierarchy actors (privilege escalation).** Hierarchy mutate routes use `JwtAuthGuard` but authorization is based on **body** `requestingAdminId` / `requestingUserId` / `managerId`, not `req.user`. Omitting `requestingUserId` on `setManager` skips the admin check (`setManagerEnsureRequesterAdminIfProvided` returns `null` when the id is omitted). An attacker with any valid JWT can send another admin’s UUID and promote/demote. Unauthenticated reads: `GET eligible-for-promotion/:adminId`, `GET hierarchy/tree`, `GET :id/hierarchy`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (45–108); `apps/api/src/services/user-hierarchy.service.ts` (492–517, 871–914, 945–980) -> Action: Bind actor exclusively to `req.user`; require `AdminAuthGuard` (or volunteer-manager policy) on mutations; authenticate tree/eligible reads.

 * **Spoofable follow graph.** `POST/DELETE /api/users/:id/follow` is JWT-gated but inserts `followData.follower_id` from the body, not `req.user.userId`. Any authenticated user can create or delete follows as any other user. - File: `apps/api/src/services/user-follow.service.ts` (6–8, 19–34); `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (165–180) -> Action: Use `req.user.userId` as follower; ignore body actor ids.

 * **Chat is effectively public.** Class-level `@UseGuards(OptionalAuthGuard)` **always returns `true`** (`jwt-auth.guard.ts:338`). Guest path for conversations uses the URL `userId` (`chat.controller.ts:267–268`). Guest path for messages skips participant checks (`378–387`) and returns conversation messages. Concrete trigger: unauthenticated `GET` of another user’s conversations or any conversation’s messages. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (308–339); `apps/api/src/controllers/chat.controller.ts` (46, 250–270, 378–387) -> Action: Require `JwtAuthGuard`; always enforce participant membership from `req.user`.

 * **Rides: no auth; spoofable passenger; unauthenticated booking status.** `rides.controller.ts` has **zero** `@UseGuards`. `POST :id/book` uses body `passenger_id` (email / firebase_uid / UUID). `PUT bookings/:bookingId/status` updates status from the body with no driver/admin check. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (37, 374–468, 505–555) -> Action: Add `JwtAuthGuard`; bind passenger to `req.user`; restrict status changes to driver/admin; reject approve when remaining seats would go negative (see Bugs).

 * **Unauthenticated mutating admin/CRM/community/items surfaces.** No `@UseGuards` on: `CrmController` (`/api/crm`), `CommunityMembersController` (`/api/community-members`, includes `contact_info` email/phone), `ItemsDeliveryController` (`/api/items-delivery`, client `owner_id`), `DedicatedItemsController` (`/api/dedicated-items` create/update/delete), `ItemsController` (`/api/collections/:collection` CRUD allowlist includes `users`, `chats`, `messages`, `tasks`, `notifications`, `rides`, `organizations`). Concrete trigger: unauthenticated `POST /api/crm`, `DELETE /api/community-members/:id`, `POST /api/collections/messages/...`. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/crm.controller.ts`; `community-members.controller.ts`; `items-delivery.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/dedicated-items.controller.ts`; `apps/api/src/items/items.controller.ts` + `items.service.ts` (15–36) -> Action: Auth + role/ownership on all mutating routes; lock down or remove generic collections CRUD.

 * **Unauthenticated Redis session/token store access (non-production).** `RedisTestController` (`/redis-test`) has no guards. `app.module.ts:122` registers it when `NODE_ENV !== "production"`. `railway.toml.dev` sets `NODE_ENV = "development"`, so the **dev Railway service** exposes `GET /redis-test/keys?pattern=*`, `GET /redis-test/get/:key`, `POST /redis-test/set`, `DELETE /redis-test/delete/:key` against the session/token store. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/redis-test.controller.ts` (16–124); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (122) -> Action: Remove from all deployed builds (including development) or gate with `AdminAuthGuard` + local-only bind.

 * **Unauthenticated rate-limit bypass.** `DELETE /rate-limit/clear` (and test/stats/rules/custom/simulate) has no auth. An attacker can clear their own (or any) identifier’s throttle. Combined with missing global `APP_GUARD`, SRS §3.1 “global 60 req/60s via ThrottlerGuard” is not actually enforced. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rate-limit.controller.ts` (20–175); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77) — `ThrottlerModule.forRoot` present, **no `APP_GUARD` / `ThrottlerGuard` registration anywhere in `apps/api/src`** -> Action: Register global `ThrottlerGuard`; delete or admin-gate the debug controller.

 * **Unauthenticated session enumeration and forced logout.** Demo `SessionController` (`/session`) has no guards. `POST /session/login` verifies real `password_hash` via argon2. `GET /session/user/:userId` lists sessions; `DELETE /session/logout/:sessionId` and `DELETE /session/logout-all/:userId` destroy sessions without proving possession. Concrete trigger: `DELETE /session/logout-all/<victim-uuid>` signs the victim out of all devices. - File: `apps/api/src/auth/controllers/session.controller.ts` (27–229) -> Action: Remove from production/dev deployments; if kept for tests, bind to `JwtAuthGuard` + ownership and disable login.

 * **Unauthenticated Google Places proxy (quota / cost).** `PlacesController` has no guards. `GET /autocomplete`, `GET /place-details`, and `GET /search-stats` call Google with `process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY` (or dump Redis search-term analytics). Concrete trigger: unauthenticated high-volume `GET /autocomplete` or `/place-details` burns billed quota and can probe the key’s validity via error bodies. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/places.controller.ts` (9–87, 88–103) -> Action: Require auth (or a tight per-IP throttle + app-check); never expose the raw Google error/key status; consider Maps SDK on-device instead of a server proxy for public traffic.

 * **Request-path and startup `DROP TABLE … CASCADE`.** `GET /api/posts` calls `ensurePostsTable()` → `migrateExistingPostsTableColumns`; if `id` or `author_id` is missing, it runs `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS posts CASCADE` on a user request. Task side-effects call `TasksSchemaService.ensurePostsTable()` which drops `posts` when `author_id` is absent. `DatabaseInit.onModuleInit` drops `donations` / `rides` if a legacy JSONB `data` column exists. Concrete trigger: schema drift or a malicious/failed migration that removes a column, then any public GET posts or API boot wipes related tables. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (67–78, 176–199, 830–842); `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks-schema.service.ts` (18–41); `apps/api/src/database/database.init.ts` (568, 670) -> Action: Move DDL to versioned migrations; never DROP on a request path; refuse boot on unexpected schema instead of destroying data.

 * **Committed credentials and backups.** Tracked `apps/api/railway.toml.dev` contains a live-format `JWT_SECRET`. Runbook `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md:126` embeds a production-labeled `JWT_SECRET` (value not repeated here). 49 JSON files under `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/` are tracked; `user_profiles.json` schema includes `email`, `phone`, `password_hash`. - File: `apps/api/railway.toml.dev:34`; `docs/SSOT/runbooks/api/environment-separation.md:126`; `apps/api/data-backups/20251224-162421/*` -> Action: Rotate all exposed JWT secrets; purge secrets from git history; gitignore backups; use secret managers only.

 * **JWT-gated but unscoped task list.** `GET /api/tasks` uses `JwtAuthGuard` then lists by query filters (`assignee`, `status`, …) with **no** restriction to the caller’s tasks. Any authenticated user can enumerate all tasks (ClosedRoute even queries another profile’s `assignee`). - File: `apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.controller.ts` (74–146) -> Action: Default-scope to `req.user`; require admin for global lists.

 * **Unthrottled primary mobile login/register.** SRS §2.1.2 and §3.1 require `ThrottlerGuard` on auth. `AuthController` (`/auth/*`) has class-level `ThrottlerGuard`, but the **primary mobile path** (`apiService.ts` / `dbConfig.USER_LOGIN`) calls `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` on `UsersController`, which have **no** throttle and no global `APP_GUARD`. Concrete trigger: unbounded credential stuffing against `/api/users/login` (and account-creation spam on `/api/users/register`) bypasses the documented 60 req/60s auth limit. - File: `apps/api/src/controllers/users.controller.ts` (111–118); `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (72–77) -> Action: Apply `ThrottlerGuard` (stricter login tier) to `/api/users/login` and `/register`; register global `APP_GUARD`; keep `/auth/*` and `/api/users/*` auth surfaces consistent.

---

## 🐞 Bugs & Logic Issues

 * **Ride booking approval can oversell seats.** `updateBookingStatus` sets status to `approved` then only marks the ride `full` when remaining ≤ 0; no capacity reject and no `FOR UPDATE` row lock before approve. Concurrent approvals can exceed `available_seats`. - Recommendation: In one transaction, lock the ride + sum approved seats; reject approve when `seats_requested` would exceed remaining capacity. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/rides.controller.ts` (505–555).

 * **Task done PATCH can create duplicate completion posts.** `evaluateMarkDoneTimeLogRequirement` skips when `oldStatus === "done"`, but `runUpdateTaskCompletionPosts` still runs whenever `body.status === "done"` (`tasks-patch-mutation.service.ts:380`) with no transition check. - Recommendation: Gate completion side effects on `oldStatus !== "done"`; add an idempotent unique constraint / upsert for completion posts.

 * **Auth refresh waiters hang on failure.** `apps/mobile/auth/interceptors/authFetchInterceptor.ts` (~75–89): failed refresh clears `refreshSubscribers` without resolving waiters subscribed via `subscribeTokenRefresh`. Parallel 401s wait forever. - Recommendation: Reject/resolve all subscribers on failure paths before clearing tokens.

 * **ClosedRoute task type inconsistency.** `ClosedRoute.tsx` filters existing content with `type === 'task'` (~324) but pushes `type: 'task_post'` (~351), so duplicate suppression against post-derived tasks is ineffective. - Recommendation: Align filter and push types (or normalize via a single type constant matching `FeedItem.type`).

 * **Spoofable post comment author.** `POST /api/posts/:postId/comments` is JWT-gated but uses body `user_id`, not `req.user.userId`. Any authenticated user can attribute comments to another account. - Recommendation: Bind `user_id` exclusively from the JWT. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (1573–1584).

 * **Unauthenticated knowledge-link writes.** `POST /api/donations/knowledge/links` is explicitly public and inserts caller-supplied URL/display name (optional spoofed `createdByUserId`). Spam/defacement surface. DELETE is JWT-gated and **does** check `admin` / `super_admin` / `org_admin` in-handler (312–318) — decorator alone is not admin. - Recommendation: Require auth; rate-limit; ignore client `createdByUserId`. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/donations.controller.ts` (226–291, 304–318).

 * **`PUT /api/posts/:postId` owner check falls back to body `user_id`.** Guard sets `req.user.userId` (JWT payload), so the fallback is currently dead in the happy path; if the payload shape ever omitted `userId`/`id`/`sub`, a caller could pass another user’s id and pass the `author_id` check. - Recommendation: Remove `body.user_id` fallback; compare `req.user.userId` only. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/posts.controller.ts` (2283–2332). (The 2026-05-04 `images` TEXT[] mapping in commit `073722f` is otherwise correct.)

 * **Unauthenticated stats analytics / real-time (new 2026-08-16).** `GET /api/stats/analytics/categories` has no guard and returns `SUM` of money donations plus weekly/monthly counts. `GET /api/stats/real-time` has no guard and returns last-hour `user_activities` counts plus `users_online` (profiles with `last_active` in 5 minutes). `GET /api/stats/analytics/users` is correctly `AdminAuthGuard`. Concrete trigger: unauthenticated scrape of donation-money totals and live activity. - Recommendation: Auth or admin-gate money/activity analytics; keep only coarse public community counters unauthenticated. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/stats.controller.ts` (439–496, 782–831).

 * **`SYNC_API_KEY` defaults to `change-me-in-production`.** Sync mutate routes also use `AdminAuthGuard`, so this is not a standalone bypass today, but a missing env var leaves a known default and the extra key check is skipped when the default is still in place. - Recommendation: Fail closed if unset in production. File: `apps/api/src/controllers/sync.controller.ts` (27–40, 422–424, 511–513).

---

## 🏗️ Architecture & Clean Code

 * **Clean Architecture layers are absent.** No `/domain`, `/application`, or `/infrastructure` folders under `apps/` or `packages/` (count 0). Target in `docs/SSOT/CODE_QUALITY/layers-and-boundaries.md` (Infrastructure → Application → Domain inward) is not met. Domain logic lives in Nest controllers/services that import `pg` `Pool`, Redis, and HTTP decorators.

 * **Closest pure islands** (no Nest/`pg` imports): `apps/api/src/tasks/task-completion-participants.ts`, `tasks-validation.ts`, `tasks-query-params.ts`, `tasks.types.ts`. `tasks.controller.ts` still injects `Pool`.

 * **Fat HTTP/SQL controllers** (far above the ~200 LOC guideline in `constraints.md`): `posts.controller.ts` 2741, `stats.controller.ts` 1709, `user-hierarchy.service.ts` 1675, `database.init.ts` 1374, `community-group-challenges.controller.ts` 1097, `donations.controller.ts` 892, `chat.controller.ts` 813, `rides.controller.ts` 779. `items-delivery.service.ts` remains misplaced under `controllers/`.

 * **Mobile debt hubs:** `utils/databaseService.ts` (1225), `apiService.ts` (1055), `enhancedDatabaseService.ts` (897); duplicate `google_auth/SecureApiService.ts` (1165). Duplicate feed mappers (`mapPostToFeedItemForItemsScreen` / `ForTrumpScreen` vs `useFeedData`) remain logged as follow-ups in `tech-debt-log.md`. **196** TypeScript source files exceed ~200 LOC (apps+packages, excluding `node_modules`/`dist`).

 * **SRS vs code (unchanged):** Operator matching module, anonymity_level column, and `/api/operator/*` still missing (`docs/SSOT/SRS/10-gaps-and-assumptions.md`). OptionalAuthGuard’s always-true behavior contradicts SRS §3.2 (“enriches authenticated requests” while allowing guests — it does not protect private chat). Dual login surfaces (`/auth/login` throttled vs `/api/users/login` unthrottled) contradict SRS §2.1.2.

 * **Hardcoded root-admin email in request-path SQL.** `user-profile.service.ts` special-cases `karmacommunity2.0@gmail.com` in SELECT/UPDATE (lines 134, 181, 281–289, 519–524, 566–571) instead of `ROOT_ADMIN_EMAIL` / `kc-root-admin.defaults.ts`. Contradicts SRS §3.2 SEC-003.1 (“Admin access is role-based only — no hardcoded email checks”).

Registry totals remain non-deterministic for this lockfile (historical range ~45–96). Action: pin/upgrade resolving parents; report observed range, not a single count.

 * **`ThrottlerModule` without global `APP_GUARD`** — comments claim global 60 req/min; only Auth + Notifications apply `ThrottlerGuard`. Matches SRS §10.3 “No rate limiting on all endpoints.” Primary mobile login is therefore unthrottled (see Critical).

 * **Admin files list is JWT-only.** `GET` admin files / folders use `JwtAuthGuard` without `AdminAuthGuard` (`admin-files.controller.ts` 80–140); any authenticated user can list shared admin files. Mutations correctly require admin.

 * **Request-path SQL** uses parameterized `$N` in app controllers; XSS patterns (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` / runtime `eval(`) absent under `apps/` (only a comment in `parse-all-users-literal.js`). Offline scripts still interpolate table names (`import-data.ts` / `export-data.ts`).

# 🛡️ Daily Audit Report: 2026-08-15

**Product HEAD audited:** `3d92f9d9cfbf7fa4e02b763eeb62a131d494f040` (merge #101 — identical to `origin/dev` and to the 2026-08-14 audit)
**Audit branch:** `cursor/daily-repository-audit-fc71`
**Scope:** Holistic security / bug / architecture / debt re-verification; secret, DDL, auth-guard, XSS/SQLi, npm, unguarded-controller, and login-throttle scans
**SRS mapping:** NFR §3.1–3.2 (`docs/SSOT/SRS/03-non-functional-requirements.md`); auth §2.1, users §2.2, rides §2.4, posts §2.5, tasks, chat §2.7 (`docs/SSOT/SRS/functional/*`); backend §5; gaps §10
**Mode:** Report-only (no product code fixes in this pass — blast radius of confirmed issues is multi-module; prior daily-audit PRs including #130 remain unmerged to `dev`)
**Delta vs 2026-08-14:** No product commits. All previously confirmed critical items re-verified at the same line numbers. New first-class item: unthrottled primary mobile login/register on `POST /api/users/login` and `POST /api/users/register` (ThrottlerGuard exists only on `/auth/*` and notifications; no global `APP_GUARD`). Places finding expanded to include unauthenticated `/place-details` and `/search-stats`. npm audit totals again non-deterministic (**45 → 95 → 96**; critical still **4**).
